```sql
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_warranty_redeliver
    CHECK (need_redeliver = FALSE OR repair_status <> 'REPAIRED'
           OR redeliver_no IS NOT NULL OR repair_end_at IS NULL);

-- 维修天数非负
ALTER TABLE m10_warranty
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_warranty_repair_days
    CHECK (repair_days IS NULL OR (repair_days >= 0 AND repair_days <= 90));

-- 费用快照非负
ALTER TABLE m10_warranty
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_warranty_fee
    CHECK (fee_amount_snap >= 0);

-- 需要审批的必须有审批状态
ALTER TABLE m10_warranty
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_warranty_approval
    CHECK (need_approval = FALSE OR approval_status IS NOT NULL);

-- 审批通过必须有审批人与时间
ALTER TABLE m10_warranty
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_warranty_approved
    CHECK (approval_status IS NULL OR approval_status <> 'APPROVED'
           OR (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL));

-- 质保卡记录不应有维修信息
ALTER TABLE m10_warranty
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_warranty_card_no_repair
    CHECK (record_type <> 'WARRANTY_CARD' OR repair_status IS NULL);

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_warranty IS '质保与维修：质保卡发放、维修单、返修、质保内/外判定、维修后重新交付';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_warranty.in_warranty IS '是否在质保期内，判定依据为 repair 受理日 <= warranty_end_date';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_warranty.judge_basis IS '判定依据（>=10 字），是费用争议与投诉处理的第一手举证';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_warranty.redeliver_no IS '返修后的取镜单号，维修后必须重新走取镜四道校验';
```

**设计要点：**

1. **为什么质保卡与维修单放在同一张表用 `record_type` 区分？**
   两者共享 90% 的字段（订单、顾客、质保范围、起止日期）。若拆两张表，「查这副眼镜的完整历史」
   要 UNION 两个表，质保卡反查维修记录还要额外关联。更重要的是：质保卡发放（`WARRANTY_CARD`）与
   维修（`REPAIR`）在业务上是**同一份质保档案的两种事件**，放一起后按 `order_id` 排序就是完整时间线，
   前台一次查询就能回答顾客「我这副眼镜什么时候买的、保多久、修过几次」。

2. **为什么 `ck_m10_warranty_in_warranty` 要把「判定结果」与「是否在保 / 是否免费」绑死？**
   质保判定是**钱的问题**：判成免费，医院承担成本；判成付费，顾客掏钱。如果允许
   `judge_result = 'FREE_REPAIR'` 而 `in_warranty = FALSE`（超保却免费），就等于给了一个「随便免」的
   后门。这条约束强制：免费必须真的在保，付费必须真的不免费。确实需要超保免费的（如质量问题、投诉
   安抚），走 `need_approval` 审批链，留痕但不改判定自洽性。

3. **为什么 `need_redeliver` 与 `redeliver_no` 要单独存在？**
   维修后眼镜回到顾客手上，**在业务上是一次新的交付**，必须重新走取镜四道校验（尤其是「加工完工」
   与「参数核对」）。若直接复用原取镜单，会丢失「维修后是否核对过」的记录，而返修后参数错配恰恰是
   最高发的投诉来源。

4. **为什么 `judge_basis` 强制 ≥ 10 字？**
   这是本表唯一要求「写字」的字段，刻意的。质保判定最容易引发争吵的场景是「这是人为损坏还是质量
   问题」，判定人当时看到的划痕形态、脱膜位置、螺丝状态，不写下来就永远说不清。强制写 10 字以上，
   让判定人必须记录观察事实，而不是只点一个按钮。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年质保卡 | 11,500 张 | 每单一张 |
| 年维修单 | 2,400 张 | 维修率 20% |
| 年合计 | 13,900 行 | — |
| 15 年总量 | 208,500 行 | — |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 560 字节 | — |
| 表大小（15 年） | 约 111 MB | 不含 TOAST 与照片 |
| 索引大小（15 年） | 约 96 MB | 12 个索引 |
| 故障照片 | 约 1.2 GB/年 | 2 张 × 250KB × 2,400，存对象存储 |

**分区：** 不分区。查询以 `order_id` / `patient_id` / `customer_phone` 为主，B-Tree 索引足够。

---

### 4.9 表：`m10_order_remind`（催取与通知记录表）

**用途：** 记录每一次给顾客发通知的**事实与结果**（到货通知、首次催取、超期催取、质保到期提醒、
维修完成通知）。这张表解决两个问题：①「我们到底通知了几次、什么时候、发到哪个号、成功没有」；
②超期纠纷时证明医院已尽提醒义务。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| order_id | BIGINT | N | — | 订单 ID（`m10_order.id`） |
| order_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 订单号冗余 |
| delivery_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 关联取镜单 ID（交付相关通知时填） |
| remind_type | VARCHAR(24) | N | — | 通知类型 `READY` 到货通知 / `PICKUP_REMIND` 催取 / `OVERDUE_WARN` 超期预警 / `OVERDUE_URGE` 超期催告 / `WARRANTY_EXPIRE` 质保到期 / `REPAIR_DONE` 维修完成 / `AMEND_NOTICE` 改单通知 |
| remind_seq | SMALLINT | N | 1 | 同一订单同一类型的第几次通知（从 1 起） |
| notify_channel | VARCHAR(16) | N | — | 通知渠道 `SMS` 短信 / `WECHAT` 微信 / `PHONE` 电话 / `APP` 应用推送（共享规范 13 节） |
| notify_target | VARCHAR(64) | N | — | 接收方（手机号脱敏后存储，如 `138****1234`） |
| template_code | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 消息模板编码（M03-05） |
| content_summary | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 内容摘要（如「您配的眼镜已到店，请携取镜单于 7 日内领取」） |
| **发送结果** | | | | |
| send_status | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'PENDING' | 发送状态 `PENDING` 待发 / `SENT` 已发送 / `DELIVERED` 已送达 / `FAILED` 失败 / `SKIPPED` 跳过 / `REPLIED` 顾客已回复 |
| send_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 发送时间 |
| delivered_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 送达时间（运营商回执） |
| fail_reason | VARCHAR(256) | Y | NULL | 失败原因（空号 / 停机 / 拒收 / 网关超时） |
| retry_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 重试次数 |
| provider_msg_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 消息服务商返回的消息 ID |
| api_request_id | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 调 M03-05 的请求追踪 ID |
| **业务上下文** | | | | |
| ready_days | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 到货天数（发送当时距 `ready_at` 的天数） |
| overdue_days | SMALLINT | Y | NULL | 超期天数（发送当时距 `pickup_deadline` 的天数） |
| is_effective | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否有效（发送后 N 天内顾客到店取镜） |
| effective_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 判定有效的时间 |
| send_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 操作人（人工通知时） |
| is_manual | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否人工触发（否则为定时任务自动） |
| remark | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 备注 |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间（分区键） |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |

**索引设计：**

| 索引名 | 类型 | 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pk_m10_order_remind` | 主键 | `id`, `created_at` | 分区表主键须含分区键 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_order` | B-Tree | `order_id`, `created_at` DESC | 按单查通知轨迹 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_no` | B-Tree | `order_no`, `created_at` | 按单号查 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_pending` | B-Tree（部分） | `send_at` WHERE `send_status IN ('PENDING','FAILED')` | 待发 / 重试队列 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_type` | B-Tree | `remind_type`, `created_at` DESC | 按类型统计（提醒效果分析） |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_seq` | 唯一（部分） | `order_id`, `remind_type`, `remind_seq` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | 防重复发送 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_effective` | B-Tree（部分） | `remind_type` WHERE `is_effective = TRUE` | 提醒有效率统计 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_provider` | B-Tree（部分） | `provider_msg_id` WHERE `provider_msg_id IS NOT NULL` | 回执对账 |
| `idx_m10_order_remind_created` | BRIN | `created_at` | 月分区范围扫描 |

**约束说明：**

```sql
-- 通知类型枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_type
    CHECK (remind_type IN ('READY', 'PICKUP_REMIND', 'OVERDUE_WARN',
                           'OVERDUE_URGE', 'WARRANTY_EXPIRE', 'REPAIR_DONE',
                           'AMEND_NOTICE'));

-- 通知渠道枚举（与共享规范 13 节 notify_channel 一致）
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_channel
    CHECK (notify_channel IN ('SMS', 'WECHAT', 'PHONE', 'APP'));

-- 发送状态枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_status
    CHECK (send_status IN ('PENDING', 'SENT', 'DELIVERED',
                           'FAILED', 'SKIPPED', 'REPLIED'));

-- 通知序号从 1 起，最多 10 次（防骚扰）
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_seq
    CHECK (remind_seq >= 1 AND remind_seq <= 10);

-- 已发送必须有发送时间
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_sent
    CHECK (send_status NOT IN ('SENT', 'DELIVERED', 'REPLIED')
           OR send_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 失败必须有原因
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_failed
    CHECK (send_status <> 'FAILED'
           OR (fail_reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(fail_reason)) >= 2));

-- 送达时间不早于发送时间
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_time
    CHECK (send_at IS NULL OR delivered_at IS NULL OR delivered_at >= send_at);

-- 重试次数范围
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_retry
    CHECK (retry_count >= 0 AND retry_count <= 5);

-- 到货通知必须有到货天数
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_ready
    CHECK (remind_type NOT IN ('READY', 'PICKUP_REMIND', 'OVERDUE_WARN',
                               'OVERDUE_URGE')
           OR ready_days IS NOT NULL);

-- 超期类通知必须有超期天数
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_overdue
    CHECK (remind_type NOT IN ('OVERDUE_WARN', 'OVERDUE_URGE')
           OR overdue_days IS NOT NULL);

-- 天数非负
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_days
    CHECK ((ready_days IS NULL OR ready_days >= 0)
           AND (overdue_days IS NULL OR overdue_days >= 0));

-- 有效必须有判定时间
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_effective
    CHECK (is_effective = FALSE OR effective_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 人工通知必须有操作人
ALTER TABLE m10_order_remind
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_remind_manual
    CHECK (is_manual = FALSE OR send_by IS NOT NULL);

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_order_remind IS '催取与通知记录：到货通知、催取、超期催告、质保到期提醒的发送事实与结果，月分区保留 24 个月';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_order_remind.notify_target IS '接收方手机号脱敏存储（138****1234），明文不落库';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_order_remind.is_effective IS '提醒是否有效：发送后到货保留期内顾客到店取镜即判定有效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_order_remind.remind_seq IS '同一订单同一类型通知的序号，用于控制催取节奏与防骚扰';
```

**分区策略：** 按 `created_at` 月度 `RANGE` 分区，保留 24 个月。

```sql
CREATE TABLE m10_order_remind (
    id                BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
    order_id          BIGINT      NOT NULL,
    order_no          VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    delivery_id       BIGINT,
    remind_type       VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
    remind_seq        SMALLINT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    notify_channel    VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
    notify_target     VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    template_code     VARCHAR(32),
    content_summary   VARCHAR(512),
    send_status       VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    send_at           TIMESTAMP,
    delivered_at      TIMESTAMP,
    fail_reason       VARCHAR(256),
    retry_count       SMALLINT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    provider_msg_id   VARCHAR(64),
    api_request_id    VARCHAR(64),
    ready_days        SMALLINT,
    overdue_days      SMALLINT,
    is_effective      BOOLEAN     NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    effective_at      TIMESTAMP,
    send_by           BIGINT,
    is_manual         BOOLEAN     NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    remark            VARCHAR(512),
    created_at        TIMESTAMP   NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at        TIMESTAMP   NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    is_deleted        BOOLEAN     NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);

CREATE TABLE m10_order_remind_202610 PARTITION OF m10_order_remind
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01') TO ('2026-11-01');
CREATE TABLE m10_order_remind_202611 PARTITION OF m10_order_remind
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11-01') TO ('2026-12-01');

CREATE INDEX idx_m10_order_remind_202610_order
    ON m10_order_remind_202610 (order_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m10_order_remind_202610_seq
    ON m10_order_remind_202610 (order_id, remind_type, remind_seq)
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
```

> **保留策略：** 24 个月在线；超期分区 `DETACH` 转冷存。分区由 APScheduler 任务
> `ensure_order_remind_partition` 每月 25 日预建。

**设计要点：**

1. **为什么 `notify_target` 存脱敏手机号而不是明文？**
   本表是**高频写入**的通知流水（年 4.6 万条），若存明文，等于在数据库里多了一份未加密的手机号
   副本，扩大泄露面。实际发送时手机号从 `m10_order.customer_phone_enc` 解密传入 M03-05，
   流水只记脱敏值用于「发给谁了」的追溯。合规审计要求「可证明通知行为」，脱敏值足以满足。

2. **为什么要有 `remind_seq` 且唯一？**
   催取是**定时任务**驱动的，最容易出的故障是「同一天发了 5 条一样的短信」——顾客投诉骚扰，医院
   被运营商限流。`(order_id, remind_type, remind_seq)` 唯一索引保证同一单同一类型的每一次通知只发一遍，
   任务重跑也不会重复发送。序号本身承载业务含义：第 1 次是到货通知，第 2~3 次是催取，第 4 次是超期
   催告。

3. **为什么要有 `is_effective` 字段？**
   这是「提醒是否真的有用」的唯一量化依据。发完短信后顾客是否到店，仅靠发送量统计不出来。任务
   `mark_remind_effective` 在每日扫「发送后 3 天内完成交付」的通知置 TRUE，月度报表就能算出「哪种
   渠道、第几次提醒最有效」，直接指导催取策略调整。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年通知量 | 46,000 条 | 人均 4 条（到货 1 + 催取 2 + 其他 1） |
| 24 个月在线 | 92,000 行 | 分区保留期 |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 380 字节 | — |
| 月分区大小 | 约 1.5 MB | 3,800 行/月 |
| 在线总量 | 约 35 MB | 不含索引 |
| 分区数 | 24 个 | 滚动创建 |

---

### 4.10 表：`m10_order_hold`（超期未取保管登记表）

**用途：** 到货超过保留期（默认 30 天）仍未取镜的订单进入保管状态，登记在册。记录保管开始与结束
时间、保管位置、保管费、催告情况、遗失责任划分。这是**责任边界**的凭据：超过保管期后眼镜在店内
遗失，责任归属取决于这张表的登记与催告记录。

| 字段名 | 类型 | 允许空 | 默认 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | BIGINT | N | IDENTITY | 主键 |
| hold_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 保管单号 `BG` + YYYYMMDD + 4 位流水 |
| order_id | BIGINT | N | — | 订单 ID（`m10_order.id`） |
| order_no | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 订单号冗余 |
| delivery_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 关联取镜单 ID（若已生成草稿取镜单） |
| patient_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 患者 ID（只读引用 M03） |
| customer_name | VARCHAR(64) | N | — | 顾客姓名快照 |
| customer_phone | VARCHAR(32) | N | — | 顾客手机号快照 |
| **保管信息** | | | | |
| ready_at | TIMESTAMP | N | — | 到货时间（冗余，计算超期天数） |
| hold_start_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 保管开始时间（= 取镜截止日次日 00:00） |
| hold_end_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 保管结束时间（取镜 / 转遗失 / 处理完毕） |
| hold_days | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 保管天数 |
| hold_location | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 保管位置（如 `A区-3号柜-2层`） |
| hold_operator_id | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 保管登记人 |
| hold_status | VARCHAR(24) | N | 'HOLDING' | 保管状态 `HOLDING` 保管中 / `PICKED_UP` 已取镜 / `LOST` 已遗失 / `DISPOSED` 已处理 / `RETURNED` 已退配 |
| **催告记录（责任举证）** | | | | |
| remind_count | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 保管期内已催告次数 |
| last_remind_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 最后催告时间 |
| last_remind_channel | VARCHAR(16) | Y | NULL | 最后催告渠道 `SMS` / `WECHAT` / `PHONE` / `APP` |
| remind_confirmed | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 顾客是否已回应确认（已回应则责任更清晰） |
| remind_confirmed_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 顾客回应时间 |
| remind_confirmed_content | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 顾客回应内容摘要 |
| **保管费（M16 真值，本模块只存单号与快照）** | | | | |
| hold_fee_rule | VARCHAR(16) | N | 'FREE' | 保管费规则 `FREE` 免费 / `PER_DAY` 按日计费 / `LUMP_SUM` 一次性 |
| hold_fee_daily_snap | NUMERIC(12,2) | N | 0 | 日保管费快照（元/天） |
| free_hold_days | SMALLINT | N | 0 | 免费保管天数（超过后开始计费） |
| hold_fee_snap | NUMERIC(14,2) | N | 0 | 累计保管费快照（元，由 M16 推送） |
| hold_fee_charged | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 保管费是否已收 |
| hold_fee_sales_order_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 保管费销售单号（M16-04） |
| hold_fee_settlement_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 保管费结算单号（M16-01） |
| hold_fee_waived | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 是否减免 |
| hold_fee_waive_approval_no | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 减免审批单号（M20） |
| **遗失责任** | | | | |
| loss_reason | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 遗失原因 `STORE_LOSS` 店内遗失 / `THEFT` 被盗 / `DAMAGED` 保管损坏 / `DISPOSED` 超期处置 |
| loss_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 遗失 / 处置时间 |
| liability_result | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 责任判定 `STORE` 医院承担 / `CUSTOMER` 顾客承担 / `SHARED` 共同承担 / `UNDETERMINED` 未定 |
| liability_basis | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 判定依据（≥ 10 字，必填） |
| compensation_amount_snap | NUMERIC(14,2) | N | 0 | 赔偿金额快照（元） |
| compensation_refund_no | VARCHAR(32) | Y | NULL | 赔偿退款单号（M16-08） |
| loss_approval_no | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 遗失处理审批单号 |
| loss_approved_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 审批人 |
| loss_approved_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 审批时间 |
| **处置结果** | | | | |
| disposition_type | VARCHAR(24) | Y | NULL | 处置方式 `DISPOSE_SCRAP` 报废 / `DONATE` 捐赠 / `RESALE` 折价处理 / `RETURN_FACTORY` 退回厂家 |
| disposition_at | TIMESTAMP | Y | NULL | 处置时间 |
| disposition_approval_no | VARCHAR(64) | Y | NULL | 处置审批单号 |
| **标准字段** | | | | |
| remark | VARCHAR(512) | Y | NULL | 备注 |
| created_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 创建人 |
| created_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 创建时间 |
| updated_by | BIGINT | Y | NULL | 更新人 |
| updated_at | TIMESTAMP | N | CURRENT_TIMESTAMP | 更新时间（触发器维护） |
| is_deleted | BOOLEAN | N | FALSE | 逻辑删除 |

**索引设计：**

| 索引名 | 类型 | 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pk_m10_order_hold` | 主键 | `id` | — |
| `uk_m10_order_hold_no` | 唯一（部分） | `hold_no` WHERE `is_deleted = FALSE` | 保管单号唯一 |
| `uk_m10_order_hold_order` | 唯一（部分） | `order_id` WHERE `hold_status = 'HOLDING'` AND `is_deleted = FALSE` | 同一订单同时只能有一条保管中的记录 |
| `idx_m10_order_hold_status` | B-Tree | `hold_status`, `hold_start_at` DESC | **P07 保管清单主力索引** |
| `idx_m10_order_hold_days` | B-Tree（部分） | `hold_days` WHERE `hold_status = 'HOLDING'` | 长期保管扫描 |
| `idx_m10_order_hold_phone` | B-Tree | `customer_phone` | 顾客来电查询 |
| `idx_m10_order_hold_start` | B-Tree | `hold_start_at` DESC | 日报 |
| `idx_m10_order_hold_loss` | B-Tree（部分） | `loss_at` WHERE `loss_reason IS NOT NULL` | 遗失案件清单 |
| `idx_m10_order_hold_fee_pending` | B-Tree（部分） | `order_id` WHERE `hold_fee_snap > 0` AND `hold_fee_charged = FALSE` | 待收保管费清单 |

**约束说明：**

```sql
-- 保管单号格式：BG + 8 位日期 + 4 位流水
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_no_format
    CHECK (hold_no ~ '^BG[0-9]{12}$');

-- 保管状态枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_status
    CHECK (hold_status IN ('HOLDING', 'PICKED_UP', 'LOST',
                           'DISPOSED', 'RETURNED'));

-- 保管费规则枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_fee_rule
    CHECK (hold_fee_rule IN ('FREE', 'PER_DAY', 'LUMP_SUM'));

-- 遗失原因枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_loss_reason
    CHECK (loss_reason IS NULL
           OR loss_reason IN ('STORE_LOSS', 'THEFT', 'DAMAGED', 'DISPOSED'));

-- 责任判定枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_liability
    CHECK (liability_result IS NULL
           OR liability_result IN ('STORE', 'CUSTOMER', 'SHARED',
                                   'UNDETERMINED'));

-- 处置方式枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_disposition
    CHECK (disposition_type IS NULL
           OR disposition_type IN ('DISPOSE_SCRAP', 'DONATE',
                                   'RESALE', 'RETURN_FACTORY'));

-- 催告渠道枚举
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_channel
    CHECK (last_remind_channel IS NULL
           OR last_remind_channel IN ('SMS', 'WECHAT', 'PHONE', 'APP'));

-- 保管天数非负
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_days
    CHECK (hold_days >= 0 AND hold_days <= 3650);

-- 保管结束必须有结束时间
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_ended
    CHECK (hold_status = 'HOLDING' OR hold_end_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 保管结束时间不早于开始时间
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_time
    CHECK (hold_end_at IS NULL OR hold_end_at >= hold_start_at);

-- 遗失必须有原因、时间、责任判定与依据、审批
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_lost
    CHECK (hold_status <> 'LOST'
           OR (loss_reason IS NOT NULL
               AND loss_at IS NOT NULL
               AND liability_result IS NOT NULL
               AND liability_basis IS NOT NULL
               AND char_length(btrim(liability_basis)) >= 10
               AND loss_approval_no IS NOT NULL
               AND loss_approved_by IS NOT NULL
               AND loss_approved_at IS NOT NULL));

-- 赔偿金额非负
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_compensation
    CHECK (compensation_amount_snap >= 0);

-- 医院承担责任必须有赔偿或明确说明
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_store_liability
    CHECK (liability_result <> 'STORE' OR compensation_amount_snap >= 0);

-- 处置必须有时间与审批
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_disposed
    CHECK (hold_status <> 'DISPOSED'
           OR (disposition_type IS NOT NULL AND disposition_at IS NOT NULL
               AND disposition_approval_no IS NOT NULL));

-- 保管费字段非负
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_fee
    CHECK (hold_fee_daily_snap >= 0 AND hold_fee_snap >= 0
           AND free_hold_days >= 0);

-- 已收保管费必须有结算单号
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_fee_charged
    CHECK (hold_fee_charged = FALSE
           OR (hold_fee_sales_order_no IS NOT NULL
               AND hold_fee_settlement_no IS NOT NULL));

-- 减免必须有审批单号
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_waived
    CHECK (hold_fee_waived = FALSE OR hold_fee_waive_approval_no IS NOT NULL);

-- 免费规则不应产生保管费
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_free
    CHECK (hold_fee_rule <> 'FREE' OR hold_fee_snap = 0);

-- 催告次数范围
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_remind
    CHECK (remind_count >= 0 AND remind_count <= 30);

-- 顾客确认必须有确认时间
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_confirmed
    CHECK (remind_confirmed = FALSE OR remind_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL);

-- 手机号格式
ALTER TABLE m10_order_hold
    ADD CONSTRAINT ck_m10_order_hold_phone
    CHECK (customer_phone ~ '^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$');

COMMENT ON TABLE m10_order_hold IS '超期未取保管登记：保管期内的催告举证、保管费、遗失责任判定与处置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_order_hold.liability_basis IS '责任判定依据（>=10 字），是遗失纠纷的核心举证';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_order_hold.remind_count IS '保管期内已催告次数，次数不足医院可能承担更多责任';
COMMENT ON COLUMN m10_order_hold.hold_fee_snap IS '累计保管费快照（元），权威值在 M16 结算单';
```

**设计要点：**

1. **为什么 `uk_m10_order_hold_order` 是部分唯一索引（`WHERE hold_status = 'HOLDING'`）？**
   同一订单只能**同时**处于一条保管中的记录。但历史上可能有多条（第一次保管 → 顾客取走后再次超期 →
   第二次保管），所以不能建普通唯一索引。部分唯一索引精确表达了「保管中」这个瞬态的唯一性，
   防止定时任务重复生成保管单导致保管费重复计算。

2. **为什么把「催告次数」`remind_count` 放在保管表而不是只查通知表？**
   `m10_order_remind` 按月分区且只保留 24 个月，而保管纠纷可能在 3 年后才发生（顾客突然来要眼镜）。
   若只依赖通知表，历史分区已归档，无法举证。保管表是**低频长期表**（年 600 行），把关键催告结论
   （次数、最后时间、渠道、顾客是否回应）固化在此，等于做了一份**永不归档的责任摘要**。

3. **为什么 `liability_result = 'STORE'` 时不强制赔偿金额 > 0？**
   医院承担责任不等于一定赔钱。常见情形是「医院承担保管责任，按原价重做一副眼镜」，赔偿方式不是
   退款而是**重新配镜**（走补单流程）。强制赔偿金额 > 0 会误伤这种场景。真正必须有的是
   `liability_basis`（为什么判医院担责，≥ 10 字）与审批单号。

4. **为什么 `hold_fee_rule = 'FREE'` 时强制 `hold_fee_snap = 0`？**
   免费保管条款下绝对不能产生费用。曾有医院因为系统自动计费，对免费保管的老年顾客收了 90 元保管费，
   引发投诉。数据库层强制「规则免费则费用为 0」，让配置与结果不可能矛盾。

**量级估算：**

| 项目 | 数值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 年保管单 | 600 单 | 到货未取率 5% |
| 15 年总量 | 9,000 行 | — |
| 单行平均长度 | 约 480 字节 | — |
| 表大小（15 年） | 约 4.3 MB | — |
| 索引大小（15 年） | 约 3.6 MB | 9 个索引 |
| 年变更次数 | 约 18,000 次 UPDATE | 每日更新保管天数与费用，日均 50 次 |

**分区：** 不分区。年 600 行，15 年 9,000 行，任何索引都够快；且需要长期在线（赔偿责任追溯期长），
分区归档反而不利于查询。

---

### 4.11 SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型定义

```python
"""M10-06 配镜订单与取镜交付 —— SQLAlchemy 2.0 模型"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Identity,
    Index, Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger, String, Text, UniqueConstraint,
    func, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.models.base import Base


class M10Order(Base):
    """配镜订单主表：业务执行档案，与 M16 销售单 1:1，金额全部为快照。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_order"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False,
                                          comment="订单号 PO+YYYYMMDD+4位")
    sales_order_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True, comment="M16 销售单号，唯一索引保证两条创建路径幂等")
    sales_order_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True, comment="M16 销售单内部 ID")
    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True, comment="患者 ID（M03，只读引用）")
    customer_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True, comment="视光顾客 ID（M10-05，只读引用）")
    customer_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    patient_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    customer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    customer_phone: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    customer_phone_enc: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(256), nullable=True, comment="手机号密文 AES-256-GCM")
    gender: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    age: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)

    # 来源与创建路径
    create_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'M10_SELF'"))
    source_event_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    optometry_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    prescription_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    fitting_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    rx_source: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)

    # 订单类型与场景
    order_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'FRAME_GLASSES'"))
    order_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, server_default=text("'WALK_IN'"))
    is_urgent: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    is_supplement: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    parent_order_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    supplement_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    store_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    store_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    dept_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    seller_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    seller_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    # 业务状态组
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'DRAFT'"),
        comment="业务子态 8 态")
    sales_order_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=True, comment="M16 财务态快照，由事件更新")
    sales_status_synced_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    pay_sub_state: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    delivery_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'NOT_READY'"))
    lab_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'NOT_STARTED'"))
    lab_job_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    need_processing: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("TRUE"))

    # 金额快照组（全部由 M16 推送，本模块不计算）
    gross_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    discount_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    total_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    paid_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    deposit_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    unpaid_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    refund_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    amount_synced_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # 工期与到货
    order_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    expected_ready_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    promised_ready_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    ready_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    ready_notified_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    pickup_deadline: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    delivered_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    deliver_method: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)

    # 质保组
    warranty_months: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    warranty_start_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    warranty_end_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    warranty_card_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    repair_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))

    # 顾客确认
    confirm_items: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    confirm_passed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    confirm_checked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    # 取消 / 退换
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    cancel_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    cancelled_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    cancelled_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    returned_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    return_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    # 备注与标准字段
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024), nullable=True)
    internal_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024), nullable=True)
    ext_attrs: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_order_no", "order_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m10_order_sales", "sales_order_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "sales_order_no IS NOT NULL AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_patient", "patient_id", text("order_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_customer", "customer_id", text("order_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("customer_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_phone", "customer_phone"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_ready", "ready_at",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "delivery_status IN ('READY_TO_PICK','NOTIFIED') "
                  "AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_overdue", "pickup_deadline",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "delivery_status IN ('READY_TO_PICK','NOTIFIED','HOLDING')")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_status", "status", text("order_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_delivery_status", "delivery_status",
              text("ready_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_store_date", "store_id", text("order_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_seller", "seller_id", text("order_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_lab_job", "lab_job_no",
              postgresql_where=text("lab_job_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_parent", "parent_order_id",
              postgresql_where=text("parent_order_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_warranty_end", "warranty_end_date",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "warranty_end_date IS NOT NULL AND status = 'DELIVERED'")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_name_trgm", "customer_name",
              postgresql_using="gin",
              postgresql_ops={"customer_name": "gin_trgm_ops"}),
        Index("idx_m10_order_expected", "expected_ready_date",
              postgresql_where=text("status IN ('PAID','IN_PRODUCTION')")),
        CheckConstraint("order_no ~ '^PO[0-9]{12}$'", name="ck_m10_order_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sales_order_no IS NULL OR sales_order_no ~ '^SO[0-9]{11}$'",
            name="ck_m10_order_sales_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','PAID','IN_PRODUCTION',"
            "'READY','DELIVERED','CANCELLED','RETURNED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sales_order_status IS NULL OR sales_order_status IN "
            "('DRAFT','PARTIAL','PAID','DELIVERED','RETURNED','CANCELLED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_sales_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "delivery_status IN ('NOT_READY','READY_TO_PICK','NOTIFIED',"
            "'PICKING','DELIVERED','HOLDING','LOST')",
            name="ck_m10_order_delivery_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "lab_status IN ('NOT_STARTED','PENDING','ASSIGNED','PROCESSING',"
            "'OUTSOURCED','QC_PENDING','QC_FAILED','COMPLETED','CANCELLED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_lab_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "order_type IN ('FRAME_GLASSES','CONTACT_LENS','ORTHO_K',"
            "'ACCESSORY_ONLY','SERVICE_ONLY')",
            name="ck_m10_order_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "create_path IN ('M10_SELF','M16_PUSH','M10_AMEND','IMPORT')",
            name="ck_m10_order_create_path"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sales_order_status IS NULL"
            " OR (status = 'DRAFT' AND sales_order_status = 'DRAFT')"
            " OR (status = 'SUBMITTED' AND sales_order_status IN ('DRAFT','PARTIAL'))"
            " OR (status = 'PAID' AND sales_order_status IN ('PARTIAL','PAID'))"
            " OR (status = 'IN_PRODUCTION' AND sales_order_status = 'PAID')"
            " OR (status = 'READY' AND sales_order_status IN ('PAID','DELIVERED'))"
            " OR (status = 'DELIVERED' AND sales_order_status IN ('PAID','DELIVERED'))"
            " OR (status = 'RETURNED' AND sales_order_status = 'RETURNED')"
            " OR (status = 'CANCELLED' AND sales_order_status = 'CANCELLED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_map"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "create_path <> 'M16_PUSH' OR sales_order_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_push_link"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('DRAFT','CANCELLED') OR sales_order_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_sales_required"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "char_length(btrim(customer_name)) >= 1"
            " AND customer_phone ~ '^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$'",
            name="ck_m10_order_customer"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'CANCELLED' OR (cancel_reason IS NOT NULL"
            " AND char_length(btrim(cancel_reason)) >= 10"
            " AND cancelled_by IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_cancel"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'DELIVERED' OR (delivered_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND warranty_start_date IS NOT NULL AND warranty_end_date IS NOT NULL"
            " AND warranty_card_no IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_delivered"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status NOT IN ('READY','DELIVERED','RETURNED') OR ready_at IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_ready"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status <> 'IN_PRODUCTION' OR (need_processing = FALSE"
            " OR lab_job_no IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_production"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "gross_amount_snap >= 0 AND discount_amount_snap >= 0"
            " AND total_amount_snap >= 0 AND paid_amount_snap >= 0"
            " AND deposit_amount_snap >= 0 AND unpaid_amount_snap >= 0"
            " AND refund_amount_snap >= 0 AND paid_amount_snap <= total_amount_snap",
            name="ck_m10_order_amounts"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sales_order_status <> 'PAID' OR paid_amount_snap >= total_amount_snap",
            name="ck_m10_order_paid_full"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_supplement = FALSE OR (parent_order_id IS NOT NULL"
            " AND parent_order_id <> id AND supplement_reason IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_supplement"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "warranty_start_date IS NULL OR warranty_end_date IS NULL"
            " OR warranty_end_date >= warranty_start_date",
            name="ck_m10_order_warranty_range"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "order_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND (ready_at IS NULL OR ready_at >= order_at)"
            " AND (delivered_at IS NULL OR delivered_at >= order_at)",
            name="ck_m10_order_time_order"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "ready_at IS NULL OR pickup_deadline IS NULL"
            " OR pickup_deadline >= ready_at::date",
            name="ck_m10_order_pickup_deadline"),
        CheckConstraint("repair_count >= 0", name="ck_m10_order_repair_count"),
    )


class M10OrderItem(Base):
    """配镜订单明细表：记眼别、光度与工序，价格仅为快照。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_order_item"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    line_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    item_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)

    product_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    brand: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    model: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    category_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    lens_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    supply_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    is_customized: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    processing_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    charge_item_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    batch_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    # 眼别与光度
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False, server_default=text("'NA'"))
    sphere: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=True)
    cylinder: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=True)
    axis: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    add_power: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=True)
    prism: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(6, 2), nullable=True)
    prism_base: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    pd_total: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    pd_right: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    pd_left: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    pd_height: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    base_curve: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    diameter: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)
    cyl_axis_stable: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    custom_params: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)

    # 数量与金额快照
    quantity: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("1"))
    unit: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    unit_price_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(12, 4), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))

    # 加工工序
    process_template_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    process_types: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    process_type_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    require_outsource: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    # 到货与交付
    arrive_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, server_default=text("'PENDING'"))
    arrived_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    delivered_in_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    delivered_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    is_delivered: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    # 质保
    warranty_months: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    serial_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    # 备注与标准字段
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    ext_attrs: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_order_item_line", "order_id", "line_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_order", "order_id", "line_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_no", "order_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_product", "product_code",
              postgresql_where=text("product_code IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_undelivered", "order_id",
              postgresql_where=text("is_delivered = FALSE AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_arrive", "arrive_status", "order_id",
              postgresql_where=text("arrive_status <> 'ARRIVED'")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_outsource", "order_id",
              postgresql_where=text("require_outsource = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_lens", "lens_type",
              postgresql_where=text("lens_type IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_serial", "serial_no",
              postgresql_where=text("serial_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_item_delivery", "delivered_in_id",
              postgresql_where=text("delivered_in_id IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("line_no >= 1 AND line_no <= 100",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_line_no"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "item_type IN ('FRAME','LENS','PROCESSING','ACCESSORY','SERVICE')",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_type"),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU','NA')",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "lens_type IS NULL OR lens_type IN ('SINGLE_VISION','PROGRESSIVE',"
            "'BIFOCAL','DEFOCUS','PRISM')",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_lens_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sphere IS NULL OR (sphere >= -30.00 AND sphere <= 30.00"
            " AND (sphere * 4) = trunc(sphere * 4))",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_sphere"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "cylinder IS NULL OR (cylinder >= -15.00 AND cylinder <= 15.00"
            " AND (cylinder * 4) = trunc(cylinder * 4))",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_cylinder"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(axis IS NULL OR (axis >= 1 AND axis <= 180))"
            " AND ((axis IS NOT NULL) OR (cylinder IS NULL OR cylinder = 0))",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_axis"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "cylinder IS NULL OR cylinder = 0 OR axis IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_axis_required"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "item_type <> 'LENS' OR eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU')",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_lens_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "pd_total IS NULL OR ((pd_right IS NULL OR pd_right > 0)"
            " AND (pd_left IS NULL OR pd_left > 0)"
            " AND (pd_right IS NULL OR pd_left IS NULL OR pd_total IS NULL"
            " OR abs(pd_total - (pd_right + pd_left)) <= 1.00))",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_pd"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(pd_total IS NULL OR (pd_total >= 40.00 AND pd_total <= 80.00))"
            " AND (pd_right IS NULL OR (pd_right >= 20.00 AND pd_right <= 45.00))"
            " AND (pd_left IS NULL OR (pd_left >= 20.00 AND pd_left <= 45.00))",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_pd_range"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "lens_type <> 'PROGRESSIVE'"
            " OR (add_power IS NOT NULL AND pd_height IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_progressive"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "lens_type <> 'PRISM' OR (prism IS NOT NULL AND prism_base IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_prism"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "prism_base IS NULL OR prism_base IN "
            "('IN','OUT','UP','DOWN','BI','BO','BU','BD')",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_prism_base"),
        CheckConstraint("quantity > 0", name="ck_m10_order_item_qty"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "item_type <> 'LENS'"
            " OR (eye_side = 'OU' AND quantity = 2)"
            " OR (eye_side IN ('OD','OS') AND quantity = 1)",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_qty_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "arrive_status IN ('PENDING','PARTIAL','ARRIVED','NOT_REQUIRED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_arrive"),
        CheckConstraint("unit_price_snap >= 0 AND amount_snap >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_amounts"),
        CheckConstraint("delivered_qty >= 0 AND delivered_qty <= quantity",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_delivered_qty"),
        CheckConstraint("arrive_status <> 'ARRIVED' OR arrived_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_arrived_at"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_delivered = FALSE OR (delivered_in_id IS NOT NULL"
            " AND delivered_qty > 0)",
            name="ck_m10_order_item_delivered"),
        CheckConstraint("is_customized = FALSE OR processing_days > 0",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_custom_days"),
        CheckConstraint("process_type_count >= 0 AND process_type_count <= 11",
                        name="ck_m10_order_item_process_count"),
    )


class M10OrderStatusLog(Base):
    """订单状态流转日志表：双状态机每次迁移留痕，按月分区。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_order_status_log"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    from_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    to_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    from_sales_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    to_sales_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    from_delivery_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=True)
    to_delivery_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=True)
    action_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    action_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    action_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    action_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    source_event_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    source_ref_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    total_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=True)
    paid_amount_snap: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=True)
    reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    changed_fields: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    elapsed_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_m10_order_status_log_order", "order_id",
              text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_status_log_no", "order_no", "created_at"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_status_log_event", "source_event_id",
              postgresql_where=text("source_event_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_status_log_action", "action_code",
              text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_status_log_to", "to_status", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_status_log_created", "created_at",
              postgresql_using="brin"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "to_status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','PAID','IN_PRODUCTION',"
            "'READY','DELIVERED','CANCELLED','RETURNED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_to"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "from_status IS NULL OR from_status IN "
            "('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','PAID','IN_PRODUCTION',"
            "'READY','DELIVERED','CANCELLED','RETURNED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_from"),
        CheckConstraint("from_status IS NULL OR from_status <> to_status",
                        name="ck_m10_order_status_log_changed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action_source IN ('USER','EVENT','TASK','API','SYSTEM')",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_source"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action_source <> 'EVENT' OR source_event_id IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_event"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action_source <> 'USER' OR action_by IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_user"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "to_status NOT IN ('CANCELLED','RETURNED')"
            " OR (reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(reason)) >= 10)",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_reason"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(total_amount_snap IS NULL OR total_amount_snap >= 0)"
            " AND (paid_amount_snap IS NULL OR paid_amount_snap >= 0)"
            " AND (elapsed_ms IS NULL OR elapsed_ms >= 0)",
            name="ck_m10_order_status_log_amounts"),
        {"postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (created_at)"},
    )


class M10OrderAmend(Base):
    """订单改单记录表：固化前后差异与差额，差额结算在 M16。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_order_amend"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    amend_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    amend_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("1"))
    amend_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    amend_reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=False)
    amend_stage: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    is_restricted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    before_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    after_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    diff_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    diff_settle_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'PENDING'"))
    diff_settlement_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    diff_refund_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    diff_settled_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    change_items: Mapped[Dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False)
    before_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    after_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)

    lab_impact: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    lab_job_no_old: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    lab_job_no_new: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    need_approval: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    approval_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    approved_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    approved_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    amend_by: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    amend_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    amended_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_order_amend_no", "amend_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m10_order_amend_seq", "order_id", "amend_seq", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_amend_order", "order_id", text("amended_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_amend_pending", "diff_settle_status",
              postgresql_where=text("diff_settle_status = 'PENDING'")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_amend_type", "amend_type", text("amended_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_amend_stage", "amend_stage", "is_restricted"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_amend_approval", "approval_status",
              postgresql_where=text("approval_status = 'PENDING'")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_amend_date", text("amended_at DESC")),
        CheckConstraint("amend_no ~ '^GD[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "amend_type IN ('CHANGE_FRAME','CHANGE_LENS','ADD_ACCESSORY',"
            "'REMOVE_ITEM','CHANGE_QTY','CHANGE_RX','CHANGE_ADDRESS','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "amend_stage IN ('BEFORE_PAID','AFTER_PAID','IN_PRODUCTION','READY')",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_stage"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "diff_settle_status IN ('PENDING','CHARGED','REFUNDED','WAIVED','NONE')",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_diff_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "lab_impact IS NULL OR lab_impact IN "
            "('NONE','REASSIGN','REWORK','SCRAP')",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_lab_impact"),
        CheckConstraint("diff_amount_snap = after_amount_snap - before_amount_snap",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_diff"),
        CheckConstraint("char_length(btrim(amend_reason)) >= 10",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_reason"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "jsonb_typeof(change_items) = 'array'"
            " AND jsonb_array_length(change_items) >= 1",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_items"),
        CheckConstraint("amend_seq >= 1 AND amend_seq <= 20",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_seq"),
        CheckConstraint("before_amount_snap >= 0 AND after_amount_snap >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_amounts"),
        CheckConstraint("diff_amount_snap <> 0 OR diff_settle_status <> 'PENDING'",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_no_diff"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "diff_settle_status <> 'CHARGED'"
            " OR (diff_settlement_no IS NOT NULL AND diff_settled_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_charged"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "diff_settle_status <> 'REFUNDED'"
            " OR (diff_refund_no IS NOT NULL AND diff_settled_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_refunded"),
        CheckConstraint("need_approval = FALSE OR approval_status IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_order_amend_approval"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "approval_status IS NULL OR approval_status <> 'APPROVED'"
            " OR (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_approved"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "amend_stage NOT IN ('IN_PRODUCTION','READY') OR is_restricted = TRUE",
            name="ck_m10_order_amend_restricted"),
    )


class M10Delivery(Base):
    """取镜交付主表：取镜单 QJ，承载四道校验、签字举证与拍照留存。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_delivery"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    delivery_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    sales_order_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    delivery_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("1"))
    is_partial: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    delivery_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, server_default=text("'DRAFT'"))

    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    customer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    customer_phone: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    pickup_person_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    pickup_person_phone: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    pickup_person_relation: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    pickup_person_id_card: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)

    check_paid: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    check_paid_detail: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    check_lab_done: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    check_lab_detail: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    check_all_arrived: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    check_arrived_detail: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    check_customer_confirm: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    check_confirm_detail: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    check_passed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    check_failed_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    checked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    checked_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)

    sign_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    signature_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    signature_hash: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)
    paper_scan_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    signed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    signer_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    photo_package_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    photo_product_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    photo_id_card_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    photo_hash: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)
    photo_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))

    deliver_method: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, server_default=text("'IN_STORE'"))
    store_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    store_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    express_company: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    express_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    receiver_address: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)

    warranty_card_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    warranty_card_printed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    warranty_months: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    warranty_start_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    warranty_end_date: Mapped[Optional[date]] = mapped_column(Date, nullable=True)
    care_guide_given: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    followup_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    delivered_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    delivered_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    delivered_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    cancel_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    cancelled_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    cancelled_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_delivery_no", "delivery_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m10_delivery_seq", "order_id", "delivery_seq", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_order", "order_id", "delivery_seq"),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_no", "order_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_date", text("delivered_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_store", "store_id", text("delivered_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_operator", "delivered_by", text("delivered_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_warranty", "warranty_card_no",
              postgresql_where=text("warranty_card_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_unchecked", "order_id",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "check_passed = FALSE AND delivery_status <> 'CANCELLED'")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_person_phone", "pickup_person_phone",
              postgresql_where=text("pickup_person_phone IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_express", "express_no",
              postgresql_where=text("express_no IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("delivery_no ~ '^QJ[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "warranty_card_no IS NULL OR warranty_card_no ~ '^WK[0-9]{12}$'",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_card_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "delivery_status IN ('DRAFT','CONFIRMED','PRINTED','SIGNED',"
            "'COMPLETED','CANCELLED')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "deliver_method IN ('IN_STORE','EXPRESS','DOOR','AGENT')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_method"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_type IS NULL OR sign_type IN "
            "('ELECTRONIC','PAPER_SCAN','NONE')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_sign_type"),
        CheckConstraint("delivery_seq >= 1 AND delivery_seq <= 10",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_seq"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_passed = FALSE OR (check_paid = TRUE AND check_lab_done = TRUE"
            " AND check_all_arrived = TRUE AND check_customer_confirm = TRUE)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_check_passed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "delivery_status = 'DRAFT' OR delivery_status = 'CANCELLED'"
            " OR (check_passed = TRUE AND checked_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND checked_by IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_confirmed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "delivery_status <> 'COMPLETED' OR (delivered_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND delivered_by IS NOT NULL AND signed_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND sign_type IS NOT NULL AND sign_type <> 'NONE')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_completed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sign_type <> 'ELECTRONIC'"
            " OR (signature_url IS NOT NULL AND signature_hash IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_esign"),
        CheckConstraint("sign_type <> 'PAPER_SCAN' OR paper_scan_url IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_psign"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "deliver_method <> 'EXPRESS'"
            " OR (express_company IS NOT NULL AND express_no IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_express"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "deliver_method NOT IN ('EXPRESS','DOOR') OR receiver_address IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_address"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "deliver_method <> 'AGENT' OR (pickup_person_name IS NOT NULL"
            " AND pickup_person_relation IS NOT NULL"
            " AND pickup_person_id_card IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_agent"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "pickup_person_relation IS NULL OR pickup_person_relation IN "
            "('SELF','FAMILY','FRIEND','AGENT')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_person_relation"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "delivery_status <> 'CANCELLED' OR (cancel_reason IS NOT NULL"
            " AND char_length(btrim(cancel_reason)) >= 5"
            " AND cancelled_by IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_cancel"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "warranty_start_date IS NULL OR warranty_end_date IS NULL"
            " OR warranty_end_date >= warranty_start_date",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_warranty_range"),
        CheckConstraint("photo_count >= 0 AND photo_count <= 20",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_photo_count"),
        CheckConstraint("customer_phone ~ '^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_phone"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "signed_at IS NULL OR delivered_at IS NULL OR signed_at <= delivered_at",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_signed_before_done"),
    )


class M10DeliveryItem(Base):
    """取镜交付明细表：记录本次实际交付行与现场参数核对结果。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_delivery_item"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    delivery_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    delivery_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_item_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    line_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    item_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    product_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    product_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    eye_side: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False, server_default=text("'NA'"))
    delivered_qty: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(10, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("1"))

    param_matched: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    param_diff: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    packaging_intact: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("TRUE"))
    appearance_ok: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("TRUE"))
    serial_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    batch_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    photo_url: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    customer_confirmed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    confirmed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    has_exception: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    exception_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    exception_action: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    exception_remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    exception_ref_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_delivery_item_line", "delivery_id", "order_item_id",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_delivery", "delivery_id", "line_no"),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_order", "order_id"),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_item", "order_item_id"),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_exception", "exception_type",
              postgresql_where=text("has_exception = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_mismatch", "delivery_id",
              postgresql_where=text("param_matched = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_serial", "serial_no",
              postgresql_where=text("serial_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_delivery_item_batch", "batch_no",
              postgresql_where=text("batch_no IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU','NA')",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_item_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint("delivered_qty > 0", name="ck_m10_delivery_item_qty"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "item_type <> 'LENS' OR eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_item_lens_eye"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exception_type IS NULL OR exception_type IN "
            "('PARAM_MISMATCH','APPEARANCE_DEFECT','WRONG_ITEM','DAMAGED','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_item_exception_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "exception_action IS NULL OR exception_action IN "
            "('REJECT','ACCEPT_WITH_DISCOUNT','REPLACE','REWORK')",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_item_exception_action"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "has_exception = FALSE OR (exception_type IS NOT NULL"
            " AND exception_action IS NOT NULL"
            " AND char_length(btrim(exception_remark)) >= 5)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_item_exception"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "param_matched = TRUE OR (param_diff IS NOT NULL"
            " AND jsonb_typeof(param_diff) = 'array'"
            " AND jsonb_array_length(param_diff) >= 1)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_item_param_diff"),
        CheckConstraint("param_matched = TRUE OR has_exception = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_item_mismatch"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "has_exception = TRUE OR (exception_type IS NULL"
            " AND exception_action IS NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_delivery_item_no_exception"),
        CheckConstraint("customer_confirmed = FALSE OR confirmed_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_delivery_item_confirmed"),
    )


class M10Warranty(Base):
    """质保与维修表：质保卡发放、维修单、质保判定与维修后重新交付。"""

    warranty_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)

    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    delivery_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)

    order_item_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)

    customer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)

    repair_start_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(

    repair_end_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    repair_operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    repair_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)

    need_redeliver: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(

    redelivered_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    followup_plan_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)

    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    approval_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    approved_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    handled_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
```
