```python
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_warranty_no", "warranty_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_order", "order_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_card", "warranty_no",
              postgresql_where=text("record_type = 'WARRANTY_CARD'")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_patient", "patient_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_phone", "customer_phone"),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_expiring", "warranty_end_date",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "record_type = 'WARRANTY_CARD' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_repair_pending", "repair_status",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "repair_status IN ('PENDING','REPAIRING','OUTSOURCED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_judge_pending", "created_at",
              postgresql_where=text("judge_result = 'PENDING'")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_type", "record_type", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_redeliver", "redeliver_no",
              postgresql_where=text("redeliver_no IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_warranty_fault", "fault_type",
              postgresql_where=text("fault_type IS NOT NULL")),
        CheckConstraint("warranty_no ~ '^WK[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_warranty_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "record_type IN ('WARRANTY_CARD','REPAIR','REWORK','REPLACE','RECALL')",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_record_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "warranty_scope IS NULL OR warranty_scope IN "
            "('LENS','FRAME','COATING','WHOLE')",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_scope"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "fault_type IS NULL OR fault_type IN "
            "('SCRATCH','COATING_PEEL','FRAME_DEFORM','FRAME_BROKEN',"
            "'SCREW_LOOSE','NOSE_PAD','LENS_CRACK','OTHER')",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_fault_type"),
        CheckConstraint("fault_eye_side IN ('OD','OS','OU','NA')",
                        name="ck_m10_warranty_eye_side"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "judge_result IS NULL OR judge_result IN "
            "('FREE_REPAIR','PAID_REPAIR','FREE_REPLACE','REJECT','PENDING')",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_judge"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "repair_status IS NULL OR repair_status IN "
            "('PENDING','REPAIRING','OUTSOURCED','REPAIRED','FAILED')",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_repair_status"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "repair_type IS NULL OR repair_type IN "
            "('POLISH','ADJUST','RESOLDER','REPLACE_PART','RELENS','RESCREW')",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_repair_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "record_type <> 'WARRANTY_CARD' OR (warranty_start_date IS NOT NULL"
            " AND warranty_end_date IS NOT NULL AND warranty_months IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_card_dates"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "warranty_start_date IS NULL OR warranty_end_date IS NULL"
            " OR warranty_end_date >= warranty_start_date",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_dates"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "record_type NOT IN ('REPAIR','REWORK')"
            " OR (fault_type IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(fault_desc)) >= 5)",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_repair_fault"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "judge_result IS NULL OR judge_result = 'PENDING'"
            " OR (judge_by IS NOT NULL AND judge_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND judge_basis IS NOT NULL"
            " AND char_length(btrim(judge_basis)) >= 10)",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_judged"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "judge_result IS NULL"
            " OR ((judge_result IN ('FREE_REPAIR','FREE_REPLACE')"
            " AND in_warranty = TRUE AND is_free = TRUE)"
            " OR (judge_result = 'PAID_REPAIR'"
            " AND is_free = FALSE AND fee_amount_snap >= 0))",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_in_warranty"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_free = TRUE OR judge_result <> 'PAID_REPAIR'"
            " OR charge_item_code IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_paid"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "repair_status <> 'REPAIRED' OR (repair_end_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND repair_operator_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_repaired"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "repair_start_at IS NULL OR repair_end_at IS NULL"
            " OR repair_end_at >= repair_start_at",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_repair_time"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "need_redeliver = FALSE OR repair_status <> 'REPAIRED'"
            " OR redeliver_no IS NOT NULL OR repair_end_at IS NULL",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_redeliver"),
        CheckConstraint("repair_days IS NULL OR (repair_days >= 0"
                        " AND repair_days <= 90)",
                        name="ck_m10_warranty_repair_days"),
        CheckConstraint("fee_amount_snap >= 0", name="ck_m10_warranty_fee"),
        CheckConstraint("need_approval = FALSE OR approval_status IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_warranty_approval"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "approval_status IS NULL OR approval_status <> 'APPROVED'"
            " OR (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_warranty_approved"),
        CheckConstraint("record_type <> 'WARRANTY_CARD' OR repair_status IS NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_warranty_card_no_repair"),
    )


class M10OrderRemind(Base):
    """催取与通知记录表：通知发送事实与结果，按月分区。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_order_remind"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    delivery_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    remind_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False)
    remind_seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("1"))
    notify_channel: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    notify_target: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=False, comment="脱敏手机号 138****1234")
    template_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    content_summary: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    send_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, server_default=text("'PENDING'"))
    send_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    delivered_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    fail_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)
    retry_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    provider_msg_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    api_request_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    ready_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    overdue_days: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=True)
    is_effective: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    effective_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    send_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    is_manual: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_order", "order_id", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_no", "order_no", "created_at"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_pending", "send_at",
              postgresql_where=text("send_status IN ('PENDING','FAILED')")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_type", "remind_type", text("created_at DESC")),
        Index("uk_m10_order_remind_seq", "order_id", "remind_type", "remind_seq",
              unique=True, postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_effective", "remind_type",
              postgresql_where=text("is_effective = TRUE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_provider", "provider_msg_id",
              postgresql_where=text("provider_msg_id IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_remind_created", "created_at",
              postgresql_using="brin"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "remind_type IN ('READY','PICKUP_REMIND','OVERDUE_WARN',"
            "'OVERDUE_URGE','WARRANTY_EXPIRE','REPAIR_DONE','AMEND_NOTICE')",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_type"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "notify_channel IN ('SMS','WECHAT','PHONE','APP')",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_channel"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "send_status IN ('PENDING','SENT','DELIVERED','FAILED',"
            "'SKIPPED','REPLIED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_status"),
        CheckConstraint("remind_seq >= 1 AND remind_seq <= 10",
                        name="ck_m10_order_remind_seq"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "send_status NOT IN ('SENT','DELIVERED','REPLIED') OR send_at IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_sent"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "send_status <> 'FAILED' OR (fail_reason IS NOT NULL"
            " AND char_length(btrim(fail_reason)) >= 2)",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_failed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "send_at IS NULL OR delivered_at IS NULL OR delivered_at >= send_at",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_time"),
        CheckConstraint("retry_count >= 0 AND retry_count <= 5",
                        name="ck_m10_order_remind_retry"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "remind_type NOT IN ('READY','PICKUP_REMIND','OVERDUE_WARN',"
            "'OVERDUE_URGE') OR ready_days IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_ready"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "remind_type NOT IN ('OVERDUE_WARN','OVERDUE_URGE')"
            " OR overdue_days IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_overdue"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(ready_days IS NULL OR ready_days >= 0)"
            " AND (overdue_days IS NULL OR overdue_days >= 0)",
            name="ck_m10_order_remind_days"),
        CheckConstraint("is_effective = FALSE OR effective_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_order_remind_effective"),
        CheckConstraint("is_manual = FALSE OR send_by IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_order_remind_manual"),
        {"postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (created_at)"},
    )


class M10OrderHold(Base):
    """超期未取保管登记表：保管期催告举证、保管费与遗失责任判定。"""

    __tablename__ = "m10_order_hold"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, Identity(), primary_key=True)
    hold_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    order_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    order_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    delivery_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    patient_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    customer_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    customer_phone: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)

    ready_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    hold_start_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    hold_end_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    hold_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    hold_location: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    hold_operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    hold_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(24), nullable=False, server_default=text("'HOLDING'"))

    remind_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    last_remind_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    last_remind_channel: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=True)
    remind_confirmed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    remind_confirmed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    remind_confirmed_content: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)

    hold_fee_rule: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, server_default=text("'FREE'"))
    hold_fee_daily_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(12, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    free_hold_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    hold_fee_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    hold_fee_charged: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    hold_fee_sales_order_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    hold_fee_settlement_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    hold_fee_waived: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))
    hold_fee_waive_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)

    loss_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    loss_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    liability_result: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    liability_basis: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    compensation_amount_snap: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(14, 2), nullable=False, server_default=text("0"))
    compensation_refund_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    loss_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    loss_approved_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    loss_approved_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)

    disposition_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=True)
    disposition_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=True)
    disposition_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    created_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    updated_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.current_timestamp())
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, server_default=text("FALSE"))

    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uk_m10_order_hold_no", "hold_no", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("uk_m10_order_hold_order", "order_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "hold_status = 'HOLDING' AND is_deleted = FALSE")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_hold_status", "hold_status",
              text("hold_start_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_hold_days", "hold_days",
              postgresql_where=text("hold_status = 'HOLDING'")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_hold_phone", "customer_phone"),
        Index("idx_m10_order_hold_start", text("hold_start_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_hold_loss", "loss_at",
              postgresql_where=text("loss_reason IS NOT NULL")),
        Index("idx_m10_order_hold_fee_pending", "order_id",
              postgresql_where=text(
                  "hold_fee_snap > 0 AND hold_fee_charged = FALSE")),
        CheckConstraint("hold_no ~ '^BG[0-9]{12}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "hold_status IN ('HOLDING','PICKED_UP','LOST','DISPOSED','RETURNED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_status"),
        CheckConstraint("hold_fee_rule IN ('FREE','PER_DAY','LUMP_SUM')",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_fee_rule"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "loss_reason IS NULL OR loss_reason IN "
            "('STORE_LOSS','THEFT','DAMAGED','DISPOSED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_loss_reason"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "liability_result IS NULL OR liability_result IN "
            "('STORE','CUSTOMER','SHARED','UNDETERMINED')",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_liability"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "disposition_type IS NULL OR disposition_type IN "
            "('DISPOSE_SCRAP','DONATE','RESALE','RETURN_FACTORY')",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_disposition"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "last_remind_channel IS NULL OR last_remind_channel IN "
            "('SMS','WECHAT','PHONE','APP')",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_channel"),
        CheckConstraint("hold_days >= 0 AND hold_days <= 3650",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_days"),
        CheckConstraint("hold_status = 'HOLDING' OR hold_end_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_ended"),
        CheckConstraint("hold_end_at IS NULL OR hold_end_at >= hold_start_at",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_time"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "hold_status <> 'LOST' OR (loss_reason IS NOT NULL"
            " AND loss_at IS NOT NULL AND liability_result IS NOT NULL"
            " AND liability_basis IS NOT NULL"
            " AND char_length(btrim(liability_basis)) >= 10"
            " AND loss_approval_no IS NOT NULL"
            " AND loss_approved_by IS NOT NULL AND loss_approved_at IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_lost"),
        CheckConstraint("compensation_amount_snap >= 0",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_compensation"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "hold_status <> 'DISPOSED' OR (disposition_type IS NOT NULL"
            " AND disposition_at IS NOT NULL"
            " AND disposition_approval_no IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_disposed"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "hold_fee_daily_snap >= 0 AND hold_fee_snap >= 0"
            " AND free_hold_days >= 0",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_fee"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "hold_fee_charged = FALSE OR (hold_fee_sales_order_no IS NOT NULL"
            " AND hold_fee_settlement_no IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_fee_charged"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "hold_fee_waived = FALSE OR hold_fee_waive_approval_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m10_order_hold_waived"),
        CheckConstraint("hold_fee_rule <> 'FREE' OR hold_fee_snap = 0",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_free"),
        CheckConstraint("remind_count >= 0 AND remind_count <= 30",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_remind"),
        CheckConstraint("remind_confirmed = FALSE OR remind_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_confirmed"),
        CheckConstraint("customer_phone ~ '^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$'",
                        name="ck_m10_order_hold_phone"),
    )
```

---

### 4.12 Pydantic Schema 定义

```python
"""M10-06 配镜订单与取镜交付 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""

from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

OrderStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "SUBMITTED", "PAID", "IN_PRODUCTION",
                      "READY", "DELIVERED", "CANCELLED", "RETURNED"]
SalesOrderStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "PARTIAL", "PAID", "DELIVERED",
                           "RETURNED", "CANCELLED"]
DeliveryStatus = Literal["NOT_READY", "READY_TO_PICK", "NOTIFIED",
                         "PICKING", "DELIVERED", "HOLDING", "LOST"]
DeliverySheetStatus = Literal["DRAFT", "CONFIRMED", "PRINTED", "SIGNED",
                              "COMPLETED", "CANCELLED"]
LabStatus = Literal["NOT_STARTED", "PENDING", "ASSIGNED", "PROCESSING",
                    "OUTSOURCED", "QC_PENDING", "QC_FAILED", "COMPLETED",
                    "CANCELLED"]
OrderType = Literal["FRAME_GLASSES", "CONTACT_LENS", "ORTHO_K",
                    "ACCESSORY_ONLY", "SERVICE_ONLY"]
CreatePath = Literal["M10_SELF", "M16_PUSH", "M10_AMEND", "IMPORT"]
ItemType = Literal["FRAME", "LENS", "PROCESSING", "ACCESSORY", "SERVICE"]
EyeSide = Literal["OD", "OS", "OU", "NA"]
LensType = Literal["SINGLE_VISION", "PROGRESSIVE", "BIFOCAL",
                   "DEFOCUS", "PRISM"]
SupplyType = Literal["STOCK", "CUSTOM"]
ProcessType = Literal["EDGING", "CUTTING", "MOUNTING", "GROOVING",
                      "DRILLING", "POLISHING", "TINTING", "COATING",
                      "REINFORCE", "ADJUSTING", "CLEANING"]
AmendType = Literal["CHANGE_FRAME", "CHANGE_LENS", "ADD_ACCESSORY",
                    "REMOVE_ITEM", "CHANGE_QTY", "CHANGE_RX",
                    "CHANGE_ADDRESS", "OTHER"]
AmendStage = Literal["BEFORE_PAID", "AFTER_PAID", "IN_PRODUCTION", "READY"]
DiffSettleStatus = Literal["PENDING", "CHARGED", "REFUNDED", "WAIVED", "NONE"]
LabImpact = Literal["NONE", "REASSIGN", "REWORK", "SCRAP"]
DeliverMethod = Literal["IN_STORE", "EXPRESS", "DOOR", "AGENT"]
SignType = Literal["ELECTRONIC", "PAPER_SCAN", "NONE"]
PickupRelation = Literal["SELF", "FAMILY", "FRIEND", "AGENT"]
WarrantyRecordType = Literal["WARRANTY_CARD", "REPAIR", "REWORK",
                             "REPLACE", "RECALL"]
WarrantyScope = Literal["LENS", "FRAME", "COATING", "WHOLE"]
FaultType = Literal["SCRATCH", "COATING_PEEL", "FRAME_DEFORM",
                    "FRAME_BROKEN", "SCREW_LOOSE", "NOSE_PAD",
                    "LENS_CRACK", "OTHER"]
JudgeResult = Literal["FREE_REPAIR", "PAID_REPAIR", "FREE_REPLACE",
                      "REJECT", "PENDING"]
RepairStatus = Literal["PENDING", "REPAIRING", "OUTSOURCED", "REPAIRED", "FAILED"]
RemindType = Literal["READY", "PICKUP_REMIND", "OVERDUE_WARN", "OVERDUE_URGE",
                     "WARRANTY_EXPIRE", "REPAIR_DONE", "AMEND_NOTICE"]
NotifyChannel = Literal["SMS", "WECHAT", "PHONE", "APP"]
HoldStatus = Literal["HOLDING", "PICKED_UP", "LOST", "DISPOSED", "RETURNED"]
HoldFeeRule = Literal["FREE", "PER_DAY", "LUMP_SUM"]
LiabilityResult = Literal["STORE", "CUSTOMER", "SHARED", "UNDETERMINED"]


# ==================== 通用校验函数 ====================

def _check_quarter_step(value: Optional[Decimal], low: Decimal,
                        high: Decimal, name: str) -> Optional[Decimal]:
    """校验度数为 0.25D（四分之一屈光度）的整数倍且在范围内。"""
    if value is None:
        return value
    if value < low or value > high:
        raise ValueError("%s 必须在 %s ~ %s 之间" % (name, low, high))
    if (value * 4) != (value * 4).to_integral_value():
        raise ValueError("%s 必须是 0.25 的整数倍" % name)
    return value


# ==================== 请求体：订单开立 ====================

class OrderItemIn(BaseModel):
    """订单明细行入参。"""

    line_no: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=100, description="行号，从 1 起")
    item_type: ItemType = Field(..., description="明细类型")
    product_code: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=32, description="商品编码（M01-08），服务类可空")
    charge_item_code: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=32, description="收费项目编码（M01-06），服务类必填")
    quantity: Decimal = Field(Decimal("1"), gt=0, description="数量，镜片按片计")
    unit: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=16, description="单位：片/副/个/次")
    eye_side: EyeSide = Field("NA", description="眼别 OD/OS/OU/NA")
    lens_type: Optional[LensType] = Field(None, description="镜片类型")
    supply_type: Optional[SupplyType] = Field(None, description="供货类型 STOCK/CUSTOM")
    sphere: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="球镜度（D），如 -4.25")
    cylinder: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="柱镜度（D），如 -0.75")
    axis: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=180, description="轴位（°）1~180")
    add_power: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="近附加（D），渐进片必填")
    prism: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="棱镜度（△）")
    prism_base: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=8, description="棱镜底向")
    pd_total: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="总瞳距（mm）")
    pd_right: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="右眼单眼瞳距（mm）")
    pd_left: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="左眼单眼瞳距（mm）")
    pd_height: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="瞳高（mm），渐进片必填")
    base_curve: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="基弧（mm）")
    diameter: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="直径（mm）")
    process_types: Optional[List[ProcessType]] = Field(
        None, description="需执行工序类型数组")
    serial_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="防伪码")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="行备注")

    @field_validator("sphere", "cylinder", "add_power")
    @classmethod
    def validate_power(cls, v: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """屈光度必须 0.25D 步长。"""
        return _check_quarter_step(v, Decimal("-30.00"), Decimal("30.00"), "屈光度")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_axis_rule(self) -> "OrderItemIn":
        """柱镜为 0 时不可填轴位；柱镜非 0 时必须填轴位。"""
        has_cyl = self.cylinder is not None and self.cylinder != 0
        if has_cyl and self.axis is None:
            raise ValueError("柱镜非 0 时必须填写轴位")
        if not has_cyl and self.axis is not None:
            raise ValueError("柱镜为 0 或未填时不可填写轴位")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_lens_eye(self) -> "OrderItemIn":
        """镜片行必须有眼别，且数量与眼别一致。"""
        if self.item_type != "LENS":
            return self
        if self.eye_side not in ("OD", "OS", "OU"):
            raise ValueError("镜片行眼别必须为 OD/OS/OU")
        if self.eye_side == "OU" and self.quantity != 2:
            raise ValueError("镜片双眼（OU）数量必须为 2")
        if self.eye_side in ("OD", "OS") and self.quantity != 1:
            raise ValueError("镜片单眼（OD/OS）数量必须为 1")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_pd(self) -> "OrderItemIn":
        """单眼瞳距之和与总瞳距差值不得超过 1mm。"""
        if self.pd_total is None or self.pd_right is None or self.pd_left is None:
            return self
        if abs(self.pd_total - (self.pd_right + self.pd_left)) > Decimal("1.00"):
            raise ValueError("单眼瞳距之和与总瞳距相差超过 1mm，请重新测量")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_progressive(self) -> "OrderItemIn":
        """渐进片必须有近附加与瞳高。"""
        if self.lens_type == "PROGRESSIVE":
            if self.add_power is None:
                raise ValueError("渐进多焦镜片必须填写近附加")
            if self.pd_height is None:
                raise ValueError("渐进多焦镜片必须填写瞳高")
        return self


class OrderCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """新建配镜订单（P03 订单开立）。"""

    patient_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="患者 ID，纯顾客可空")
    customer_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="视光顾客 ID")
    customer_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64,
                               description="顾客姓名，必填")
    customer_phone: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$",
                                description="手机号 11 位，必填")
    gender: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=8, description="性别 M/F/U")
    age: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=0, le=150, description="年龄（岁）")
    optometry_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                        description="验光单号，下单必带")
    prescription_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                           description="配镜处方号")
    fitting_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                      description="验配单号（隐形/OK镜）")
    order_type: OrderType = Field("FRAME_GLASSES", description="订单类型")
    order_source: str = Field("WALK_IN", max_length=16, description="顾客来源")
    is_urgent: bool = Field(False, description="是否加急")
    is_supplement: bool = Field(False, description="是否补单")
    parent_order_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                           description="补单时原订单号")
    supplement_reason: Optional[Literal["SINGLE_EYE", "BROKEN", "LOST",
                                        "URGENT_EXTRA", "REPLACE"]] = Field(
        None, description="补单原因")
    store_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="门店 / 院区 ID")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="开单科室 ID")
    seller_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="销售员 ID")
    expected_ready_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="预计到货日")
    promised_ready_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="承诺到货日")
    items: List[OrderItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=50,
                                     description="明细行，至少 1 行")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")
    internal_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024,
                                           description="内部备注")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_supplement(self) -> "OrderCreateIn":
        """补单必须指明原单号与原因。"""
        if self.is_supplement and not self.parent_order_no:
            raise ValueError("补单必须填写原订单号")
        if self.is_supplement and not self.supplement_reason:
            raise ValueError("补单必须填写补单原因")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_unique_line(self) -> "OrderCreateIn":
        """行号不得重复。"""
        nos = [it.line_no for it in self.items]
        if len(nos) != len(set(nos)):
            raise ValueError("明细行号重复")
        return self


class OrderUpdateIn(BaseModel):
    """修改订单草稿（仅 DRAFT 可改）。"""

    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    customer_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, min_length=1, max_length=64)
    customer_phone: Optional[str] = Field(None, pattern=r"^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$")
    promised_ready_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="承诺到货日")
    is_urgent: Optional[bool] = Field(None, description="是否加急")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024)
    internal_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024)
    items: Optional[List[OrderItemIn]] = Field(
        None, max_length=50, description="全量替换明细，不传则不修改")


class OrderQueryIn(BaseModel):
    """订单列表查询条件（P02）。"""

    keyword: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                   description="单号 / 姓名 / 手机号 / 验光单号")
    status: Optional[List[OrderStatus]] = Field(None, description="业务子态多选")
    delivery_status: Optional[List[DeliveryStatus]] = Field(
        None, description="交付状态多选")
    sales_order_status: Optional[List[SalesOrderStatus]] = Field(
        None, description="M16 财务态多选")
    order_type: Optional[List[OrderType]] = Field(None, description="订单类型多选")
    store_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="门店 ID")
    seller_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="销售员 ID")
    patient_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="患者 ID")
    date_from: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="下单日期起")
    date_to: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="下单日期止")
    ready_from: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="到货日期起")
    ready_to: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="到货日期止")
    overdue_only: bool = Field(False, description="仅查超期未取")
    page: int = Field(1, ge=1, description="页码")
    page_size: int = Field(20, ge=1, le=100, description="每页条数")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_span(self) -> "OrderQueryIn":
        """查询时间跨度不得超过一年。"""
        if self.date_from and self.date_to:
            if (self.date_to - self.date_from).days > 366:
                raise ValueError("查询时间跨度不得超过一年")
            if self.date_to < self.date_from:
                raise ValueError("结束日期不得早于开始日期")
        return self


# ==================== 请求体：改单与补单 ====================

class AmendItemChange(BaseModel):
    """单个变更项。"""

    op: Literal["ADD", "UPDATE", "REMOVE"] = Field(..., description="操作类型")
    line_no: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, description="行号，ADD 时为空")
    field: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                 description="变更字段，如 product_code")
    before: Optional[Any] = Field(None, description="变更前值")
    after: Optional[Any] = Field(None, description="变更后值")
    item: Optional[OrderItemIn] = Field(None, description="ADD 时的新明细")


class OrderAmendIn(BaseModel):
    """改单申请（P04）。"""

    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    amend_type: AmendType = Field(..., description="改单类型")
    amend_reason: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=512,
                              description="改单原因，不少于 10 字")
    changes: List[AmendItemChange] = Field(..., min_length=1,
                                           description="变更项列表")
    accept_rework: bool = Field(
        False, description="加工中改单时是否接受返工（需质检退货）")
    rework_confirm_no: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=64, description="返工确认单号（质检退货单）")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_changes(self) -> "OrderAmendIn":
        """ADD 操作必须带明细，UPDATE/REMOVE 必须带行号。"""
        for ch in self.changes:
            if ch.op == "ADD" and ch.item is None:
                raise ValueError("ADD 操作必须提供新明细")
            if ch.op in ("UPDATE", "REMOVE") and ch.line_no is None:
                raise ValueError("UPDATE/REMOVE 操作必须提供行号")
        return self


class AmendDiffSettleIn(BaseModel):
    """改单差额确认。"""

    diff_settle_status: DiffSettleStatus = Field(..., description="差额处理结果")
    diff_settlement_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                              description="补收结算单号")
    diff_refund_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                          description="退差退款单号")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_refs(self) -> "AmendDiffSettleIn":
        """补收与退差必须带对应单号。"""
        if self.diff_settle_status == "CHARGED" and not self.diff_settlement_no:
            raise ValueError("补收必须提供结算单号")
        if self.diff_settle_status == "REFUNDED" and not self.diff_refund_no:
            raise ValueError("退差必须提供退款单号")
        return self


class SupplementCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """补单申请（单眼补配 / 破损补配 / 加急补配）。"""

    parent_order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="原订单号")
    supplement_reason: Literal["SINGLE_EYE", "BROKEN", "LOST",
                               "URGENT_EXTRA", "REPLACE"] = Field(
        ..., description="补单原因")
    eye_side: Optional[EyeSide] = Field(None, description="单眼补配时的眼别")
    is_urgent: bool = Field(False, description="是否加急")
    items: List[OrderItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1, description="补配明细")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_single_eye(self) -> "SupplementCreateIn":
        """单眼补配必须指明眼别，且不能是双眼。"""
        if self.supplement_reason == "SINGLE_EYE":
            if self.eye_side is None:
                raise ValueError("单眼补配必须指定眼别")
            if self.eye_side == "OU":
                raise ValueError("单眼补配的眼别不能是双眼")
        return self


# ==================== 请求体：取镜交付 ====================

class DeliveryCheckIn(BaseModel):
    """取镜四道校验（P05 校验按钮）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="订单号")
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="校验操作员，取自登录态")


class DeliveryItemIn(BaseModel):
    """交付明细入参（含现场参数核对结果）。"""

    order_item_id: int = Field(..., description="订单明细 ID")
    delivered_qty: Decimal = Field(Decimal("1"), gt=0, description="本次交付数量")
    param_matched: bool = Field(..., description="包装参数与订单参数是否一致")
    param_diff: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = Field(
        None, description="参数差异明细，param_matched=False 时必填")
    packaging_intact: bool = Field(True, description="包装是否完好")
    appearance_ok: bool = Field(True, description="外观是否合格")
    serial_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="防伪码")
    batch_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="批次号")
    photo_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="包装照片")
    exception_type: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=24,
                                          description="异常类型")
    exception_action: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=24,
                                            description="异常处理方式")
    exception_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_mismatch(self) -> "DeliveryItemIn":
        """参数不符必须记录差异。"""
        if not self.param_matched and not self.param_diff:
            raise ValueError("参数不符时必须记录差异明细")
        return self


class DeliveryCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """创建取镜单（P05）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="订单号")
    is_partial: bool = Field(False, description="是否部分交付")
    items: List[DeliveryItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1,
                                        description="本次交付明细分行")
    deliver_method: DeliverMethod = Field("IN_STORE", description="交付方式")
    pickup_person_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                              description="取镜人姓名")
    pickup_person_phone: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                               description="取镜人手机")
    pickup_person_relation: Optional[PickupRelation] = Field(
        None, description="取镜人关系")
    pickup_person_id_card: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                                 description="取镜人证件号")
    express_company: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64)
    express_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64)
    receiver_address: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_agent(self) -> "DeliveryCreateIn":
        """他人代取必须提供代取人信息。"""
        if self.deliver_method == "AGENT":
            if not self.pickup_person_name or not self.pickup_person_relation:
                raise ValueError("他人代取必须填写代取人姓名与关系")
            if not self.pickup_person_id_card:
                raise ValueError("他人代取必须登记代取人证件号")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_express(self) -> "DeliveryCreateIn":
        """快递必须有公司与单号与地址。"""
        if self.deliver_method == "EXPRESS":
            if not self.express_company or not self.express_no:
                raise ValueError("快递交付必须填写快递公司与单号")
            if not self.receiver_address:
                raise ValueError("快递交付必须填写收货地址")
        return self


class DeliverySignIn(BaseModel):
    """取镜签字确认与举证材料上传（P05 提交）。"""

    delivery_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="取镜单号")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")
    sign_type: SignType = Field(..., description="签字方式")
    signature_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                         description="电子签名图片地址")
    paper_scan_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                          description="纸质签字扫描件地址")
    signer_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64, description="签字人")
    photo_package_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                             description="镜片包装照片")
    photo_product_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                             description="成品眼镜照片")
    photo_id_card_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                             description="取镜人证件照片")
    care_guide_given: bool = Field(False, description="是否已告知护理要点")
    confirm_note: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_sign(self) -> "DeliverySignIn":
        """签字方式与举证材料必须匹配。"""
        if self.sign_type == "ELECTRONIC" and not self.signature_url:
            raise ValueError("电子签名必须上传签名图片")
        if self.sign_type == "PAPER_SCAN" and not self.paper_scan_url:
            raise ValueError("纸质签字必须上传扫描件")
        if self.sign_type == "NONE":
            raise ValueError("取镜交付必须签字，不允许无签字")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_photo(self) -> "DeliverySignIn":
        """镜片包装照片为交付举证必需项。"""
        if not self.photo_package_url:
            raise ValueError("交付必须留存镜片包装照片")
        return self


# ==================== 请求体：质保与维修 ====================

class WarrantyRepairIn(BaseModel):
    """发起维修申请（P06）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="订单号")
    order_item_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="故障行，整副为空")
    fault_type: FaultType = Field(..., description="故障类型")
    fault_desc: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512,
                            description="故障描述，不少于 5 字")
    fault_eye_side: EyeSide = Field("NA", description="故障眼别")
    fault_images: Optional[List[str]] = Field(None, max_length=6,
                                              description="故障照片地址")
    customer_request: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=256,
                                            description="顾客诉求")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)


class WarrantyJudgeIn(BaseModel):
    """质保判定（P06）。"""

    warranty_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="维修单号")
    is_quality_issue: bool = Field(..., description="是否质量问题（非人为）")
    judge_result: JudgeResult = Field(..., description="判定结果")
    judge_basis: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=512,
                             description="判定依据，不少于 10 字")
    is_free: Optional[bool] = Field(None, description="是否免费，由判定结果推导")
    fee_amount: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0,
                                          description="维修费（元），付费时填")
    charge_item_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                            description="维修收费项目编码")
    need_redeliver: bool = Field(True, description="维修后是否需重新交付")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_paid(self) -> "WarrantyJudgeIn":
        """付费维修必须有收费项目与金额。"""
        if self.judge_result == "PAID_REPAIR":
            if not self.charge_item_code:
                raise ValueError("付费维修必须指定收费项目")
            if self.fee_amount is None:
                raise ValueError("付费维修必须填写维修金额")
        return self

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_free(self) -> "WarrantyJudgeIn":
        """免费判定不允许带金额。"""
        if self.judge_result in ("FREE_REPAIR", "FREE_REPLACE"):
            if self.fee_amount and self.fee_amount > 0:
                raise ValueError("免费质保处理不得收取费用")
        return self


class WarrantyRepairCompleteIn(BaseModel):
    """维修完成。"""

    warranty_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="维修单号")
    repair_type: Literal["POLISH", "ADJUST", "RESOLDER", "REPLACE_PART",
                         "RELENS", "RESCREW"] = Field(..., description="维修方式")
    repair_operator_id: int = Field(..., description="维修技师 ID")
    need_redeliver: bool = Field(True, description="是否需要重新交付")
    repair_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)


# ==================== 请求体：催取与保管 ====================

class RemindSendIn(BaseModel):
    """手动催取（P07）。"""

    order_nos: List[str] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100,
                                 description="订单号列表")
    notify_channel: NotifyChannel = Field("SMS", description="通知渠道")
    template_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="消息模板")
    custom_content: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512,
                                          description="自定义内容，使用模板时为空")


class HoldRegisterIn(BaseModel):
    """超期未取保管登记（P07）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="订单号")
    hold_location: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64,
                               description="保管位置，如 A区-3号柜-2层")
    hold_fee_rule: HoldFeeRule = Field("FREE", description="保管费规则")
    hold_fee_daily: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0"), ge=0, description="日保管费（元）")
    free_hold_days: int = Field(0, ge=0, le=365, description="免费保管天数")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_fee(self) -> "HoldRegisterIn":
        """按日计费必须配置日费率。"""
        if self.hold_fee_rule == "PER_DAY" and self.hold_fee_daily <= 0:
            raise ValueError("按日计费规则必须配置大于 0 的日保管费")
        return self


class HoldLossIn(BaseModel):
    """保管遗失登记与责任判定。"""

    hold_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="保管单号")
    loss_reason: Literal["STORE_LOSS", "THEFT", "DAMAGED", "DISPOSED"] = Field(
        ..., description="遗失原因")
    liability_result: LiabilityResult = Field(..., description="责任判定")
    liability_basis: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=512,
                                 description="责任判定依据，不少于 10 字")
    compensation_amount: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0"), ge=0,
                                         description="赔偿金额（元）")
    approval_no: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64,
                             description="遗失处理审批单号")


# ==================== 请求体：内部契约 ====================

class FromSalesOrderIn(BaseModel):
    """由 M16 销售单反建 M10 业务档案（P-13）。"""

    sales_order_no: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="M16 销售单号")
    sales_order_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="M16 销售单内部 ID")
    source_event_id: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=64, description="触发事件 ID，用于事件幂等")
    patient_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="患者 ID")
    customer_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="视光顾客 ID")
    customer_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=64,
                               description="顾客姓名")
    customer_phone: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^1[3-9][0-9]{9}$",
                                description="顾客手机号")
    optometry_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="验光单号")
    prescription_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="处方号")
    fitting_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32, description="验配单号")
    order_type: OrderType = Field("FRAME_GLASSES", description="订单类型")
    store_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="门店 ID")
    seller_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="销售员 ID")
    total_amount: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0,
                                            description="应收总额快照（元）")
    paid_amount: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0,
                                           description="已收金额快照（元）")
    deposit_amount: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0,
                                              description="定金快照（元）")
    sales_order_status: Optional[SalesOrderStatus] = Field(
        None, description="M16 财务态")
    items: List[OrderItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1,
                                     description="M16 销售单明细映射")


class OnDeliveredIn(BaseModel):

    operator_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="交付操作员")

    hold_end_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="保管结束时间")
    hold_days: int = Field(..., description="保管天数")
    hold_location: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="保管位置")
    hold_status: str = Field(..., description="保管状态")
    remind_count: int = Field(0, description="已催告次数")
    last_remind_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="最后催告时间")
    remind_confirmed: bool = Field(False, description="顾客是否回应")
    hold_fee_rule: str = Field(..., description="保管费规则")
    hold_fee_snap: Decimal = Field(..., description="累计保管费快照（元）")
    hold_fee_charged: bool = Field(False, description="保管费是否已收")
    liability_result: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="责任判定")
    compensation_amount_snap: Decimal = Field(..., description="赔偿金额快照（元）")


# ==================== 响应：内部契约 ====================

class InternalOrderOut(BaseModel):
    """取配镜订单业务档案出参（P-12）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., description="配镜订单号")
    sales_order_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="M16 销售单号")
    status: str = Field(..., description="业务子态")
    sales_order_status: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="M16 财务态快照")
    delivery_status: str = Field(..., description="交付状态")
    lab_status: str = Field(..., description="加工状态快照")
    lab_job_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="加工单号")
    patient_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="患者 ID")
    customer_name: str = Field(..., description="顾客姓名")
    customer_phone_masked: str = Field(..., description="脱敏手机号")
    order_type: str = Field(..., description="订单类型")
    total_amount_snap: Decimal = Field(..., description="应收总额快照（元）")
    paid_amount_snap: Decimal = Field(..., description="已收金额快照（元）")
    paid_full: bool = Field(..., description="是否已付清")
    expected_ready_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="预计到货日")
    ready_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="到货时间")
    pickup_deadline: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="取镜截止日")
    delivered_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="交付时间")
    warranty_card_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="质保卡号")
    warranty_end_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="质保到期日")
    repair_count: int = Field(0, description="维修次数")
    is_supplement: bool = Field(False, description="是否补单")
    parent_order_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="原订单号")
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list, description="明细行")
    updated_at: datetime = Field(..., description="档案更新时间")


class DeliveryStatusOut(BaseModel):
    """取镜状态查询出参（P-14）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., description="配镜订单号")
    delivery_status: str = Field(..., description="交付状态")
    delivery_status_text: str = Field(..., description="交付状态中文")
    can_pick: bool = Field(..., description="当前是否可立即取镜")
    check_passed: bool = Field(..., description="四道校验是否通过")
    blocking_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="不可取镜的卡点原因")
    blocking_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="卡点错误码")
    paid_full: bool = Field(..., description="是否已付清")
    unpaid_amount_snap: Decimal = Field(..., description="待收金额快照（元）")
    lab_status: str = Field(..., description="加工状态快照")
    lab_progress: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(
        None, description="加工进度摘要")
    all_arrived: bool = Field(..., description="是否全部到货")
    pending_lines: List[Dict[str, Any]] = Field(
        default_factory=list, description="未到货明细行")
    ready_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="到货时间")
    ready_days: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="到货天数")
    pickup_deadline: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="取镜截止日")
    is_overdue: bool = Field(False, description="是否超期")
    hold_status: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="保管状态")
    delivered_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="交付时间")
    delivery_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="取镜单号")
    next_action_text: str = Field(..., description="给前台的下一步动作建议")


class FromSalesOrderOut(BaseModel):
    """反建业务档案出参（P-13）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., description="生成的配镜订单号")
    sales_order_no: str = Field(..., description="M16 销售单号")
    is_idempotent: bool = Field(..., description="是否命中幂等（已存在返回原档案）")
    status: str = Field(..., description="业务子态")
    item_count: int = Field(..., description="明细行数")
    total_amount_snap: Decimal = Field(..., description="应收总额快照（元）")
    create_path: str = Field(..., description="创建路径 M16_PUSH")
    created_at: datetime = Field(..., description="创建时间")


class OnDeliveredOut(BaseModel):
    """取镜完成通知出参（P-15）。"""

    order_no: str = Field(..., description="配镜订单号")
    delivery_no: str = Field(..., description="取镜单号")
    delivered_at: datetime = Field(..., description="交付时间")
    order_status: str = Field(..., description="更新后的业务子态")
    delivery_status: str = Field(..., description="更新后的交付状态")
    warranty_card_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="质保卡号")
    warranty_end_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="质保到期日")
    followup_plan_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="生成/更新的复查计划号")
    is_idempotent: bool = Field(..., description="是否重复通知（幂等返回）")
    notified_systems: List[str] = Field(
        default_factory=list, description="已推送的下游系统")


class PageOut(BaseModel):
    """统一分页出参。"""

    items: List[OrderBriefOut] = Field(default_factory=list, description="数据行")
    total: int = Field(..., description="总条数")
    page: int = Field(..., description="当前页码")
    page_size: int = Field(..., description="每页条数")
    pages: int = Field(..., description="总页数")


# ==================== 响应：统计 ====================

class OrderStatItem(BaseModel):
    """统计项。"""

    stat_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="统计日期")
    store_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="门店名称")
    category: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="分类（订单类型 / 状态 / 工序）")
    order_count: int = Field(0, description="订单数")
    item_count: int = Field(0, description="明细行数")
    total_amount_snap: Decimal = Field(Decimal("0"), description="金额合计快照（元）")
    delivered_count: int = Field(0, description="已交付数")
    on_time_rate: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, description="按期交付率")
    overdue_count: int = Field(0, description="超期未取数")
    amend_count: int = Field(0, description="改单数")
    repair_count: int = Field(0, description="维修数")


class OrderStatOut(BaseModel):
    """订单业务统计出参（P09）。"""

    date_from: date = Field(..., description="统计开始日")
    date_to: date = Field(..., description="统计结束日")
    summary: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, description="汇总指标")
    by_status: List[OrderStatItem] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                           description="按业务子态分组")
    by_type: List[OrderStatItem] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                         description="按订单类型分组")
    by_store: List[OrderStatItem] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                          description="按门店分组")
    by_day: List[OrderStatItem] = Field(default_factory=list, description="按日趋势")
    delivery_quality: Dict[str, Any] = Field(
        default_factory=dict, description="交付质量指标（参数不符率等）")
    backup_note: str = Field("", description="统计口径说明")
```

## 附录 A：表清单速查

| # | 表名 | 中文名 | 主键 | 关键唯一索引 | 分区 | 年量级 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `m10_order` | 配镜订单主表 | `id` | `uk_m10_order_no`、`uk_m10_order_sales` | 否 | 12,000 |
| 2 | `m10_order_item` | 配镜订单明细表 | `id` | `uk_m10_order_item_line` | 否 | 62,000 |
| 3 | `m10_order_status_log` | 订单状态流转日志表 | `id` + `created_at` | 无（仅增不改） | 月 RANGE | 180,000 |
| 4 | `m10_order_amend` | 订单改单记录表 | `id` | `uk_m10_order_amend_no`、`uk_m10_order_amend_seq` | 否 | 3,600 |
| 5 | `m10_delivery` | 取镜交付主表 | `id` | `uk_m10_delivery_no`、`uk_m10_delivery_seq` | 否 | 11,500 |
| 6 | `m10_delivery_item` | 取镜交付明细表 | `id` | `uk_m10_delivery_item_line` | 否 | 58,000 |
| 7 | `m10_warranty` | 质保与维修表 | `id` | `uk_m10_warranty_no` | 否 | 13,900 |
| 8 | `m10_order_remind` | 催取与通知记录表 | `id` + `created_at` | `uk_m10_order_remind_seq` | 月 RANGE | 46,000 |
| 9 | `m10_order_hold` | 超期未取保管登记表 | `id` | `uk_m10_order_hold_no`、`uk_m10_order_hold_order` | 否 | 600 |

**总容量（15 年）：** 约 1.5 GB（含索引），照片与签名文件存对象存储约 10 GB/年。

## 附录 B：状态枚举汇总

### B.1 枚举字典

| 枚举 | 字段 | 取值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 业务子态 | `m10_order.status` | DRAFT/SUBMITTED/PAID/IN_PRODUCTION/READY/DELIVERED/CANCELLED/RETURNED | 8 态，与共享规范 13 节一致 |
| M16 财务态快照 | `m10_order.sales_order_status` | DRAFT/PARTIAL/PAID/DELIVERED/RETURNED/CANCELLED | 6 态，只读快照 |
| 交付状态 | `m10_order.delivery_status` | NOT_READY/READY_TO_PICK/NOTIFIED/PICKING/DELIVERED/HOLDING/LOST | 7 态 |
| 加工状态快照 | `m10_order.lab_status` | NOT_STARTED/PENDING/ASSIGNED/PROCESSING/OUTSOURCED/QC_PENDING/QC_FAILED/COMPLETED/CANCELLED | 9 态，来自 M10-07 |
| 付款子态 | `m10_order.pay_sub_state` | UNPAID/DEPOSIT_PAID/PAID_FULL | 3 态，由金额快照推导 |
| 订单类型 | `m10_order.order_type` | FRAME_GLASSES/CONTACT_LENS/ORTHO_K/ACCESSORY_ONLY/SERVICE_ONLY | — |
| 创建路径 | `m10_order.create_path` | M10_SELF/M16_PUSH/M10_AMEND/IMPORT | 两条主路径 + 补单 + 迁移 |
| 明细类型 | `m10_order_item.item_type` | FRAME/LENS/PROCESSING/ACCESSORY/SERVICE | — |
| 眼别 | `m10_order_item.eye_side` | OD/OS/OU/NA | 与共享规范 13 节一致 |
| 镜片类型 | `m10_order_item.lens_type` | SINGLE_VISION/PROGRESSIVE/BIFOCAL/DEFOCUS/PRISM | — |
| 供货类型 | `m10_order_item.supply_type` | STOCK/CUSTOM | — |
| 到货状态 | `m10_order_item.arrive_status` | PENDING/PARTIAL/ARRIVED/NOT_REQUIRED | — |
| 改单类型 | `m10_order_amend.amend_type` | CHANGE_FRAME/CHANGE_LENS/ADD_ACCESSORY/REMOVE_ITEM/CHANGE_QTY/CHANGE_RX/CHANGE_ADDRESS/OTHER | — |
| 改单阶段 | `m10_order_amend.amend_stage` | BEFORE_PAID/AFTER_PAID/IN_PRODUCTION/READY | 决定是否受限 |
| 差额结算状态 | `m10_order_amend.diff_settle_status` | PENDING/CHARGED/REFUNDED/WAIVED/NONE | — |
| 加工影响 | `m10_order_amend.lab_impact` | NONE/REASSIGN/REWORK/SCRAP | — |
| 取镜单状态 | `m10_delivery.delivery_status` | DRAFT/CONFIRMED/PRINTED/SIGNED/COMPLETED/CANCELLED | 6 态 |
| 交付方式 | `m10_delivery.deliver_method` | IN_STORE/EXPRESS/DOOR/AGENT | — |
| 签字方式 | `m10_delivery.sign_type` | ELECTRONIC/PAPER_SCAN/NONE | 完成态不允许 NONE |
| 取镜人关系 | `m10_delivery.pickup_person_relation` | SELF/FAMILY/FRIEND/AGENT | — |
| 交付异常类型 | `m10_delivery_item.exception_type` | PARAM_MISMATCH/APPEARANCE_DEFECT/WRONG_ITEM/DAMAGED/OTHER | — |
| 异常处理 | `m10_delivery_item.exception_action` | REJECT/ACCEPT_WITH_DISCOUNT/REPLACE/REWORK | — |
| 质保记录类型 | `m10_warranty.record_type` | WARRANTY_CARD/REPAIR/REWORK/REPLACE/RECALL | — |
| 质保范围 | `m10_warranty.warranty_scope` | LENS/FRAME/COATING/WHOLE | — |
| 故障类型 | `m10_warranty.fault_type` | SCRATCH/COATING_PEEL/FRAME_DEFORM/FRAME_BROKEN/SCREW_LOOSE/NOSE_PAD/LENS_CRACK/OTHER | — |
| 质保判定 | `m10_warranty.judge_result` | FREE_REPAIR/PAID_REPAIR/FREE_REPLACE/REJECT/PENDING | — |
| 维修状态 | `m10_warranty.repair_status` | PENDING/REPAIRING/OUTSOURCED/REPAIRED/FAILED | — |
| 维修方式 | `m10_warranty.repair_type` | POLISH/ADJUST/RESOLDER/REPLACE_PART/RELENS/RESCREW | — |
| 通知类型 | `m10_order_remind.remind_type` | READY/PICKUP_REMIND/OVERDUE_WARN/OVERDUE_URGE/WARRANTY_EXPIRE/REPAIR_DONE/AMEND_NOTICE | — |
| 通知渠道 | `m10_order_remind.notify_channel` | SMS/WECHAT/PHONE/APP | 与共享规范 13 节一致 |
| 发送状态 | `m10_order_remind.send_status` | PENDING/SENT/DELIVERED/FAILED/SKIPPED/REPLIED | — |
| 保管状态 | `m10_order_hold.hold_status` | HOLDING/PICKED_UP/LOST/DISPOSED/RETURNED | — |
| 保管费规则 | `m10_order_hold.hold_fee_rule` | FREE/PER_DAY/LUMP_SUM | — |
| 遗失原因 | `m10_order_hold.loss_reason` | STORE_LOSS/THEFT/DAMAGED/DISPOSED | — |
| 责任判定 | `m10_order_hold.liability_result` | STORE/CUSTOMER/SHARED/UNDETERMINED | — |
| 处置方式 | `m10_order_hold.disposition_type` | DISPOSE_SCRAP/DONATE/RESALE/RETURN_FACTORY | — |

### B.2 状态迁移动作编码（`m10_order_status_log.action_code`）

| 动作编码 | 含义 | 迁移（from → to） | 来源 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CREATE_ORDER` | M10 开单建草稿 | ∅ → DRAFT | USER |
| `CREATE_FROM_SALES` | M16 销售单反建 | ∅ → DRAFT / SUBMITTED | API（P-13） |
| `SUBMIT_ORDER` | 提交开单（调 M16-04 API-24） | DRAFT → SUBMITTED | USER |
| `SYNC_SALES_STATUS` | 同步 M16 财务态快照 | 同态内更新快照 | EVENT |
| `PAID_DEPOSIT` | 收到定金 | SUBMITTED → PAID | EVENT |
| `PAID_FULL` | 收齐全款 | SUBMITTED/PAID → PAID | EVENT |
| `DISPATCH_LAB` | 派工（调 M10-07 P-17） | PAID → IN_PRODUCTION | USER |
| `LAB_STARTED` | 加工开始 | 同态内更新 lab_status | EVENT |
| `LAB_QC_FAILED` | 质检不合格 | 同态内更新 lab_status | EVENT |
| `LAB_COMPLETED` | 加工完成且全部到货 | IN_PRODUCTION → READY | EVENT |
| `MARK_READY` | 无需加工直接到货 | PAID → READY | USER |
| `SEND_READY_NOTICE` | 发到货通知 | 同态内更新交付状态 | TASK |
| `DELIVER_ORDER` | 取镜交付完成 | READY → DELIVERED | API（P-15） |
| `AMEND_ORDER` | 改单（未改变业务态） | 同态内更新 | USER |
| `CANCEL_ORDER` | 取消订单 | DRAFT/SUBMITTED → CANCELLED | USER |
| `RETURN_ORDER` | 退换完成 | DELIVERED → RETURNED | EVENT |
| `HOLD_ORDER` | 超期转保管 | 同态内更新交付状态为 HOLDING | TASK |
| `LOSS_ORDER` | 保管遗失 | 同态内更新交付状态为 LOST | USER |
| `REPAIR_COMPLETED` | 维修完成 | 同态内更新维修计数 | USER |
| `RECONCILE_FIX` | 对账修正 | 任意 → 修正后态 | TASK |

---

## 附录 C：关键索引与查询

### C.1 六个最热的查询与执行计划

| # | 场景 | SQL 要点 | 命中索引 | 预估耗时 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | P07 催取清单：已到货未取 | `WHERE delivery_status IN ('READY_TO_PICK','NOTIFIED') AND ready_at <= $1` | `idx_m10_order_ready` | ≤ 15ms |
| 2 | P05 取镜三校：查未交付行 | `WHERE order_id = $1 AND is_delivered = FALSE AND is_deleted = FALSE` | `idx_m10_order_item_undelivered` | ≤ 2ms |
| 3 | P-12 按单号取档案 | `WHERE order_no = $1 AND is_deleted = FALSE` | `uk_m10_order_no` | ≤ 1ms |
| 4 | P-13 幂等校验 | `WHERE sales_order_no = $1 AND is_deleted = FALSE` | `uk_m10_order_sales` | ≤ 1ms |
| 5 | P02 订单列表（状态 + 门店 + 日期） | `WHERE store_id = $1 AND status = ANY($2) AND order_at >= $3 ORDER BY order_at DESC` | `idx_m10_order_store_date` | ≤ 40ms |
| 6 | P06 待维修清单 | `WHERE repair_status IN ('PENDING','REPAIRING','OUTSOURCED') ORDER BY created_at` | `idx_m10_warranty_repair_pending` | ≤ 8ms |

### C.2 关键查询示例

```sql
-- 查询 1：P07 催取清单（到货满 N 天未取，含是否超期）
SELECT o.id,
       o.order_no,
       o.customer_name,
       o.customer_phone,
       o.ready_at,
       (CURRENT_DATE - o.ready_at::date)                       AS ready_days,
       o.pickup_deadline,
       GREATEST(CURRENT_DATE - o.pickup_deadline, 0)           AS overdue_days,
       o.total_amount_snap,
       o.unpaid_amount_snap,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM m10_order_remind r
         WHERE r.order_id = o.id
           AND r.remind_type IN ('READY', 'PICKUP_REMIND')
           AND r.is_deleted = FALSE)                           AS remind_times
  FROM m10_order o
 WHERE o.delivery_status IN ('READY_TO_PICK', 'NOTIFIED')
   AND o.is_deleted = FALSE
   AND o.ready_at <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '3 days'
   AND o.store_id = :store_id
 ORDER BY o.ready_at ASC
 LIMIT 100;

-- 查询 2：取镜第三道校验（全部到货）
SELECT count(*)                                                        AS total_lines,
       count(*) FILTER (WHERE arrive_status = 'ARRIVED')               AS arrived_lines,
       count(*) FILTER (WHERE arrive_status = 'NOT_REQUIRED')          AS not_required_lines,
       coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
           'line_no', line_no,
           'product_name', product_name,
           'eye_side', eye_side,
           'arrive_status', arrive_status) ORDER BY line_no)
           FILTER (WHERE arrive_status = 'PENDING'), '[]'::jsonb)      AS pending_lines
  FROM m10_order_item
 WHERE order_id = :order_id
   AND is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 查询 3：P-14 取镜状态（一次查询拼装四道卡点）
SELECT o.order_no,
       o.delivery_status,
       o.status,
       o.lab_status,
       o.ready_at,
       (o.paid_amount_snap >= o.total_amount_snap)     AS paid_full,
       o.unpaid_amount_snap,
       (o.lab_status = 'COMPLETED' OR o.need_processing = FALSE) AS lab_done,
       NOT EXISTS (
           SELECT 1 FROM m10_order_item i
            WHERE i.order_id = o.id
              AND i.arrive_status = 'PENDING'
              AND i.is_deleted = FALSE)                AS all_arrived
  FROM m10_order o
 WHERE o.order_no = :order_no
   AND o.is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 查询 4：改单待处理差额汇总（P04 角标）
SELECT count(*)                     AS pending_count,
       coalesce(sum(diff_amount_snap), 0) AS pending_amount
  FROM m10_order_amend
 WHERE diff_settle_status = 'PENDING'
   AND is_deleted = FALSE;

-- 查询 5：月度交付质量指标
SELECT date_trunc('month', d.delivered_at)::date          AS stat_month,
       count(DISTINCT d.id)                               AS delivery_count,
       count(di.id)                                       AS delivery_lines,
       count(di.id) FILTER (WHERE di.param_matched = FALSE) AS mismatch_lines,
       count(di.id) FILTER (WHERE di.has_exception = TRUE)  AS exception_lines,
       round(100.0 * count(di.id) FILTER (WHERE di.param_matched = FALSE)
             / NULLIF(count(di.id), 0), 4)                AS mismatch_rate_pct
  FROM m10_delivery d
  JOIN m10_delivery_item di
    ON di.delivery_id = d.id AND di.is_deleted = FALSE
 WHERE d.delivery_status = 'COMPLETED'
   AND d.delivered_at >= :date_from
   AND d.delivered_at < :date_to
 GROUP BY 1
 ORDER BY 1;

-- 查询 6：跨表核对（应用层对账任务使用，验证已交付行与主表一致）
SELECT o.order_no,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM m10_order_item i
         WHERE i.order_id = o.id AND i.is_delivered = TRUE
           AND i.is_deleted = FALSE)                     AS item_delivered,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM m10_delivery_item di
         WHERE di.order_id = o.id AND di.is_deleted = FALSE) AS delivery_lines,
       (SELECT coalesce(sum(delivered_qty), 0) FROM m10_order_item i
         WHERE i.order_id = o.id AND i.is_deleted = FALSE)   AS qty_delivered,
       (SELECT coalesce(sum(delivered_qty), 0) FROM m10_delivery_item di
         WHERE di.order_id = o.id AND di.is_deleted = FALSE) AS qty_in_delivery
  FROM m10_order o
 WHERE o.status = 'DELIVERED'
   AND o.delivered_at >= CURRENT_DATE - 2;
```

### C.3 索引维护建议

| 索引 | 维护动作 | 频率 |
|---|---|---|
| `idx_m10_order_name_trgm` | `REINDEX INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | 季度 |
| `idx_m10_order_ready` | 监控 `pg_stat_user_indexes.idx_scan`，若长期为 0 说明催取路径变更 | 月度 |
| `m10_order_status_log` 分区索引 | 随分区 `CREATE INDEX` 自动创建 | 月 25 日 |
| `m10_order_remind` 分区索引 | 随分区 `CREATE INDEX` 自动创建 | 月 25 日 |
| 全表 | `VACUUM (ANALYZE)` 重点表 `m10_order`、`m10_order_item` | 每日 02:30 |

## 附录 D：配置参数与字典

### D.1 系统参数（`sys_config`，键前缀 `M10.ORDER.`）

| 参数编码 | 名称 | 类型 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `M10.ORDER.PICKUP_KEEP_DAYS` | 到货保留天数 | INTEGER | 30 | 超过后进入超期保管 |
| `M10.ORDER.READY_REMIND_DAYS` | 到货通知首次提醒天数 | INTEGER | 0 | 0 表示到货当日即通知 |
| `M10.ORDER.PICKUP_REMIND_DAYS` | 催取间隔天数 | INTEGER | 3 | 每 3 天催取一次 |
| `M10.ORDER.PICKUP_REMIND_MAX` | 最大催取次数 | INTEGER | 4 | 含到货通知 |
| `M10.ORDER.OVERDUE_WARN_DAYS` | 超期预警天数 | INTEGER | 7 | 超过保留期 7 天发超期催告 |
| `M10.ORDER.HOLD_FREE_DAYS` | 免费保管天数 | INTEGER | 15 | 保管期内免费天数 |
| `M10.ORDER.HOLD_FEE_DAILY` | 日保管费（元） | DECIMAL | 5.00 | 超出免费天数后按日计 |
| `M10.ORDER.HOLD_MAX_DAYS` | 最长保管天数 | INTEGER | 180 | 超过后进入处置流程 |
| `M10.ORDER.WARRANTY_DEFAULT_MONTHS` | 默认质保月数 | INTEGER | 12 | M01-08 未配时兜底 |
| `M10.ORDER.FRAME_WARRANTY_MONTHS` | 镜架默认质保月数 | INTEGER | 6 | — |
| `M10.ORDER.AMEND_AFTER_PAID_HOURS` | 付款后免费改单时限（小时） | INTEGER | 24 | 超时改单可能产生费用 |
| `M10.ORDER.AMEND_DIFF_APPROVAL` | 改单差额审批阈值（元） | DECIMAL | 500.00 | 超过需主管审批 |
| `M10.ORDER.REWORK_REQUIRE_QC` | 加工中改单是否强制质检退货 | BOOLEAN | TRUE | TRUE 时必须填 `rework_confirm_no` |
| `M10.ORDER.MAX_ITEMS` | 单单最大明细行 | INTEGER | 50 | — |
| `M10.ORDER.MAX_AMEND_TIMES` | 单单最大改单次数 | INTEGER | 20 | — |
| `M10.ORDER.SUPPLEMENT_MAX_DAYS` | 补单有效天数 | INTEGER | 365 | 超过后不再允许补单 |
| `M10.ORDER.PHOTO_REQUIRED` | 交付是否强制拍照 | BOOLEAN | TRUE | TRUE 时无照片不可完成交付 |
| `M10.ORDER.SIGN_REQUIRED` | 交付是否强制签字 | BOOLEAN | TRUE | TRUE 时无签字不可完成交付 |
| `M10.ORDER.REPAIR_APPROVAL_AMOUNT` | 免费换新审批阈值（元） | DECIMAL | 0.00 | 0 表示免费换新一律审批 |
| `M10.ORDER.STAT_LAG_DAYS` | 统计延迟天数 | INTEGER | 1 | T+1 出稳定报表 |

### D.2 消息模板（M03-05 `template_code`）

| 模板编码 | 场景 | 模板内容（示例） |
|---|---|---|
| `M10_ORDER_READY` | 到货通知 | 您配的眼镜已到店，请携取镜单到店领取，保留期 30 天。 |
| `M10_ORDER_PICKUP_REMIND` | 催取 | 您的眼镜已到店 {ready_days} 天，请尽快领取。 |
| `M10_ORDER_OVERDUE_WARN` | 超期预警 | 您的眼镜已超保留期 {overdue_days} 天，将转入保管。 |
| `M10_ORDER_OVERDUE_URGE` | 超期催告 | 您的眼镜超期 {overdue_days} 天，保管费 {fee} 元/天。 |
| `M10_ORDER_WARRANTY_EXPIRE` | 质保到期 | 您的镜片质保将于 {warranty_end_date} 到期，可预约免费检测。 |
| `M10_ORDER_REPAIR_DONE` | 维修完成 | 您的眼镜已维修完成，请到店取回。 |
| `M10_ORDER_AMEND_NOTICE` | 改单通知 | 您的订单已变更，差额 {diff_amount} 元，请到店处理。 |

### D.3 代码字典

| 字典编码 | 名称 | 取值 |
|---|---|---|
| `M10_ORDER_CANCEL_TYPE` | 取消类型 | CUSTOMER_CANCEL 顾客取消 / OUT_OF_STOCK 缺货 / PRICE_ERROR 开单错误 / DUPLICATE 重复开单 / OTHER 其他 |
| `M10_ORDER_SUPPLEMENT_REASON` | 补单原因 | SINGLE_EYE 单眼补配 / BROKEN 破损补配 / LOST 遗失补配 / URGENT_EXTRA 加急补配 / REPLACE 换新补配 |
| `M10_ORDER_REMIND_TYPE` | 通知类型 | READY 到货通知 / PICKUP_REMIND 催取 / OVERDUE_WARN 超期预警 / OVERDUE_URGE 超期催告 / WARRANTY_EXPIRE 质保到期 / REPAIR_DONE 维修完成 / AMEND_NOTICE 改单通知 |
| `M10_ORDER_FAULT_TYPE` | 故障类型 | SCRATCH 镜片划伤 / COATING_PEEL 脱膜 / FRAME_DEFORM 镜架变形 / FRAME_BROKEN 镜架断裂 / SCREW_LOOSE 螺丝松动 / NOSE_PAD 鼻托损坏 / LENS_CRACK 镜片崩边 / OTHER 其他 |
| `M10_ORDER_PRISM_BASE` | 棱镜底向 | IN 内 / OUT 外 / UP 上 / DOWN 下 / BI 底向内 / BO 底向外 / BU 底向上 / BD 底向下 |

## 附录 E：建表依赖顺序与 Alembic 迁移

### E.1 依赖顺序

```
第 0 层（外部依赖，须先于本子模块创建）
  ├── M03: mpi_patient
  ├── M01-08: md_optical_product / md_optical_process_template
  ├── M01-06: md_charge_item
  ├── M10-05: m10_customer
  ├── M16-04: m16_optical_order / m16_optical_item / m16_optical_return
  └── M10-07: m10_lab_job（本子模块只存 lab_job_no，不建表）

第 1 层（本子模块主表）
  └── m10_order

第 2 层（直接依赖 m10_order）
  ├── m10_order_item
  ├── m10_order_status_log
  ├── m10_order_amend
  ├── m10_order_remind
  └── m10_order_hold

第 3 层（依赖 m10_order + m10_order_item）
  ├── m10_delivery
  └── m10_warranty

第 4 层（依赖 m10_delivery + m10_order_item）
  └── m10_delivery_item
```

> **注意：** `m10_warranty` 同时引用 `m10_delivery.id`（首张取镜单）与 `m10_order_item.id`，
> 因此与 `m10_delivery` 同层创建；`m10_delivery_item` 最后创建。**全部表均不建物理外键**，
> 上述顺序仅为便于理解与备份恢复，Alembic 迁移中实际可按此顺序 `create_table`。

```python
    # ---------- 触发器：维护 updated_at ----------
    op.execute(
        "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_m10_order_touch_updated_at() "
        "RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$ BEGIN NEW.updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; "
        "RETURN NEW; END; $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql")
    for table in ("m10_order", "m10_order_item", "m10_order_amend",
                  "m10_delivery", "m10_delivery_item", "m10_warranty",
                  "m10_order_hold"):
        op.execute(
            "CREATE TRIGGER trg_%s_touch BEFORE UPDATE ON %s "
            "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_m10_order_touch_updated_at()"
            % (table, table))
    for table in ("m10_order_remind", "m10_order_status_log"):
        op.execute(
            "CREATE TRIGGER trg_%s_touch BEFORE UPDATE ON %s "
            "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_m10_order_touch_updated_at()"
            % (table, table))


def downgrade() -> None:
    """回滚：按依赖逆序删表。"""
    for table in ("m10_delivery_item", "m10_warranty", "m10_delivery",
                  "m10_order_hold", "m10_order_remind",
                  "m10_order_amend", "m10_order_status_log",
                  "m10_order_item", "m10_order"):
        op.execute("DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_%s_touch ON %s" % (table, table))
        op.drop_table(table)
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS trg_m10_order_touch_updated_at()")
```

### E.4 未来分区预建脚本（APScheduler `ensure_order_partition`）

```sql
-- 由定时任务每月 25 日执行，预建下月分区
DO $$
DECLARE
    v_next_start DATE := (date_trunc('month', CURRENT_DATE) + INTERVAL '1 month')::date;
    v_next_end   DATE := (date_trunc('month', CURRENT_DATE) + INTERVAL '2 month')::date;
    v_suffix     TEXT := to_char(v_next_start, 'YYYYMM');
BEGIN
    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS m10_order_status_log_%s '
        'PARTITION OF m10_order_status_log FOR VALUES FROM (%L) TO (%L)',
        v_suffix, v_next_start, v_next_end);
    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS m10_order_remind_%s '
        'PARTITION OF m10_order_remind FOR VALUES FROM (%L) TO (%L)',
        v_suffix, v_next_start, v_next_end);
    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_m10_order_status_log_%s_order '
        'ON m10_order_status_log_%s (order_id, created_at DESC)',
        v_suffix, v_suffix);
    EXECUTE format(
        'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_m10_order_remind_%s_order '
        'ON m10_order_remind_%s (order_id, created_at DESC)',
        v_suffix, v_suffix);
END $$;
```
